\rho\,\partial_{\theta}f_{2}(\theta,\psi)>-1
is more stringent than the condition

\rho\,\partial_{\varphi}f(\varphi,\psi)>-1
(see Remark
4.18
). However, since the bounds in Theorem
5
are trivially satisfied for any
fixed
\rho
, by possibly taking a larger constant
C_{7}
,
we may and do take for granted that the bounds hold for
\rho\geq\rho_{0}
.
Thus, we confine ourselves to consider
\rho\in(0,\rho_{0})
and assume that both

{\mathscr{S}}
and
{\mathscr{S}}_{2}
satisfy Hypotheses
1
–
3
.

4.8.2
A new correlation inequality

To fulfill the program outlined at the end of the previous section, we start by comparing
h_{2}
with
\overline{h}
. To this aim,
by using the expansion (
4.33
), we find useful to compare first
\langle p_{2}{\vphantom{p}}^{(n)}q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\rangle

with
\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n)}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}{\vphantom{G}}^{n}
(see (
4.40
) below).
This comparison is similar to the comparison in Proposition
4.3
,
the main difference being that the analogues of
g_{+}
,
{\mathfrak{u}}
and
g_{-}
now depend also on
\theta
.
Thus we need a correlation inequality that generalizes the previous one to this case.

The following preliminary result, proved in Appendix
B.3
, shows
that bounds analogous to those in Lemma
4.11
, which hold for functions
depending only on the slow variable, extend to functions
depending also on the fast variables, as far as the latter dependence is regular
enough.

Lemma 4.21
.

Let
{\mathfrak{p}}_{0},\ldots,{\mathfrak{p}}_{n-1}
be any functions in
{\mathscr{B}}_{\alpha_{0},3}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
such that,
for some
\gamma^{\prime}\in(0,\gamma)
,

1.

|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{p}}_{i}-1|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},3}=O(\rho)
for all
i=0,\ldots,n-1
,

2.

|{\mathfrak{p}}_{i}(\theta)|\leq 1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime}

for
|\theta|\leq\theta^{\prime}
for some
\theta^{\prime}
independent of
\rho
and
for all
i=0,\ldots,n-1
,

and set
{\mathfrak{p}}^{(n)}(\theta,\psi):={\mathfrak{p}}^{(n)}({\mathscr{S}}_{2};\theta,\psi)
, with
{\mathfrak{p}}^{(n)}({\mathscr{S}}_{2};\theta,\psi)
defined according to (
4.19
).
One has

|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{p}}^{(n)}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},3}^{-}\leq C\,(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{n},\qquad|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|\partial_{\theta}({\mathscr{S}}^{n}_{2})_{\theta}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},4}^{-}\leq C\,(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{n},\vskip-5.69046pt

(4.35)

where the constant
C
does not depend on
n
.

Remark 4.22
.

Condition
1
in Lemma
4.21
implies that

|{\mathfrak{p}}_{i}|_{\alpha_{0}}
,
|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|\partial_{\theta}{\mathfrak{p}}_{i}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2}

and

\|{\mathfrak{p}}_{i}\!-\!\langle{\mathfrak{p}}_{i}\rangle\|_{\infty}
are
O(\rho)
.

Remark 4.23
.

A comment analogous to Remark
4.13
applies also to Lemma
4.21
and the forthcoming Proposition
4.38
:
if we assume that property 2 in Lemma
4.21
holds for all
i=0,\ldots,n-1

except at most
n_{*}
values, with
n_{*}<n
independent of
n
,
then both results still hold.

We are now ready to state the new correlation inequality, which is proved in Appendix
C.2
.

Proposition 4.24
.

Let
{\mathfrak{p}}_{0},\ldots,{\mathfrak{p}}_{n-1}
be any functions in
{\mathscr{B}}_{\alpha_{0},3}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
satisfying,
for some
\gamma^{\prime}\in(0,\gamma)
, properties
1
and
2
in
Lemma
4.21
.
For any
g_{+}\in{\mathscr{B}}_{\alpha_{0}}^{+}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
and
g_{-}\in{\mathscr{B}}_{\alpha_{0},3}^{-}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
one has

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle g_{+}{\mathfrak{p}}^{(n)}g_{-}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\right\rangle-\langle g_{+}\rangle\langle{\mathfrak{p}}\rangle^{(n)}\langle g_{-}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n}\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\left(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime}\right)^{n}\Bigl((1+\alpha_{0}n)\lambda^{-\alpha_{0}n}\|\tilde{g}_{+}\|^{+}_{\alpha_{0}}\|\tilde{g}_{-}\|^{-}_{\alpha_{0}}+\rho\|g_{+}\|^{+}_{\alpha_{0}}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|g_{-}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|^{-}_{\alpha_{0},2}+\rho^{2}n\|\langle g_{+}\rangle\|_{\infty}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|g_{-}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},3}^{-}\Bigr),

where
{\mathfrak{p}}^{(n)}(\theta,\psi):={\mathfrak{p}}^{(n)}({\mathscr{S}}_{2};\theta,\psi)
and
\langle{\mathfrak{p}}\rangle^{(n)}(\theta):=\langle{\mathfrak{p}}\rangle^{(n)}(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}};\theta)
,
with
{\mathfrak{p}}^{(n)}({\mathscr{S}}_{2};\theta,\psi)
and
\langle{\mathfrak{p}}\rangle^{(n)}(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}};\theta)
defined according to (
4.35
) and
(
4.21
), respectively, and
C
is a constant independent of
n
.

Remark 4.25
.

Proposition
4.24
can be seen as a generalization of
Proposition
4.3
to functions which also depend smoothly on the slow
variable. This will be exploited in Subsection
4.8.3
to compare the averaged
map with the auxiliary map, by using that all the involved functions are
regular – i.e. at least
\alpha_{0}
-Hölder continuous for some
\alpha_{0}

independent of
\rho
– in the fast variable. The next step, to be achieved in
Subsection
4.8.4
, is to compare the auxiliary map with the translated
map, where the dependence on the fast variable is only
\alpha_{*}
-Hölder
continuous, with
\alpha_{*}=O(\rho)
.

4.8.3
Deviations of the conjugation of the auxiliary map

As discussed in Remark
4.20
, we assume, without loss of generality, that
\rho\leq\rho_{0}
and hence
that
{\mathscr{S}}_{2}
satisfies Hypotheses
1
–
3
.
Recall also that we are working with the extension of
{\mathscr{S}}
, although not explicitly indicated (see Remark
4.15
);
thus, all functions appearing in what follows refer to such an extension.

We start by comparing
h_{2}
with
\overline{h}
. By relying on the expansions (
4.33
) and (
4.23
), we may write

\hbox{}\hskip-5.69046pt\langle p_{2}\rangle^{\!(n)}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n}\!\!\!-\overline{p}^{(n)}\,\overline{q}{\circ}\overline{G}^{n}\!\!=\!\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n}\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}\langle p_{2}\rangle^{\!(i)}\!\bigl(\langle p_{2}\rangle\!-\!\overline{p}\bigr){\circ}\overline{G}^{i}\,\overline{p}^{(n-i-2)}{\circ}\overline{G}^{i+1}\!\!\!+\!\overline{p}^{(n)}\bigl(\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n}\!\!\!-\overline{q}{\circ}\overline{G}^{n}\bigr).\vskip-2.84544pt

(4.36)

We can now prove the following result.

Proposition 4.26
.

Let
h_{2}
and
\overline{h}
be defined according to (
4.31
) and (
2.21
),
respectively. For all
\theta\in{\mathcal{U}}
, one has

\bigl|\left\langle h_{2}(\theta,\cdot)-\overline{h}(\theta)\right\rangle\bigr|\leq C\rho,\qquad\qquad\left\langle(h_{2}(\theta,\cdot)-\overline{h}(\theta))^{2}\right\rangle\leq C\rho\,.

(4.37)

Proof
.
Observe that
p_{2}(\theta,\psi)=1+O(\rho)
, so that (compare with Remark
4.22
)

\|p_{2}-\langle p_{2}\rangle\|_{\infty}\leq C\rho,\qquad|p_{2}|_{\alpha_{0}}\leq C\rho,\qquad|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|\partial_{\theta}p_{2}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2}\leq C\rho\,.\vskip-2.84544pt

(4.38)

Since
p_{2}(0,\psi)=1+\rho\partial_{\theta}f_{2}(0,\psi)\leq 1-\rho
, for any
\rho^{\prime}\in(1,\rho)
there exists
\theta_{2}
such that
p_{2}(\theta,\psi)\leq 1-\rho^{\prime}
for

|\theta|\leq\theta_{2}
. Moreover, we easily check that

|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|q_{2}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},3}\leq C\rho,

(4.39)

so that we can apply Proposition
4.24
and obtain

\bigl|\langle p_{2}^{(n)}\!q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\rangle-\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n)}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n}\bigr|\leq C(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{n}\rho^{2}\,(1+\rho n),

(4.40)

where
\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n)}(\theta)
is given by (
4.21
), with
{\mathfrak{p}}_{i}=p_{2}

\forall i=0,\ldots,n-1
. Since

|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|\langle p_{2}\rangle-\overline{p}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{0,1}\leq C\rho^{2},\qquad|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|\langle q_{2}\rangle-\overline{q}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{0,1}\leq C\rho^{2},\vskip-2.84544pt

(4.41)

we have also, by (
4.36
),

\displaystyle\bigl|\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n)}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n}-\overline{p}^{(n)}\,\overline{q}{\circ}\overline{G}^{n}\bigr|\leq C(1\!-\!\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{n}\left(n\rho^{2}\|q_{2}\|_{\infty}+\|\langle q_{2}\rangle\!-\!\bar{q}\|_{\infty}\right)\leq C(1\!-\!\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{n}\rho^{2}(1\!+\!\rho n),

(4.42)

so that, combining (
4.40
) and (
4.42
), we obtain

|\langle p_{2}^{(n)}\!q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\rangle-\overline{p}^{(n)}\,\overline{q}{\circ}\overline{G}^{n}|\leq C(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{n}\rho^{2}(1+\rho n)
.
Summing over
n
gives the first bound in (
4.37
).

For the second bound, we start considering

\displaystyle\bigl(p_{2}^{(n_{1})}\!q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n_{1}}-\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{1})}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{1}}\bigr)\bigl(p_{2}^{(n_{2})}\!q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n_{2}}-\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{2})}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{2}}\bigr)

\displaystyle=p_{2}^{(n_{1})}\!q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n_{1}}\,p_{2}^{(n_{2})}\!q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n_{2}}-\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{1})}\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{2})}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{1}}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{2}}

\displaystyle-\bigl(p_{2}^{(n_{1})}\!q_{2}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n_{1}}\!-\!\langle p_{2}\rangle^{\!(n_{1})}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{1}}\bigr)\langle p_{2}\rangle^{\!(n_{2})}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{2}}-\langle p_{2}\rangle^{\!(n_{1})}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{1}}\!\bigl(p_{2}^{(n_{2})}\!q_{2}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n_{1}}\!-\!\langle p_{2}\rangle^{\!(n_{2})}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{2}}\bigr),

and observe that, thanks to (
4.40
),
the averages of both contributions in the last two lines
are bounded by
C\rho^{3}(1+\rho(n_{1}+n_{2}))(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{n_{1}+n_{2}}
.
As to the contribution in the second line, assuming
n_{1}\leq n_{2}
, we can write

p_{2}^{(n_{1})}(\theta,\psi)\,q_{2}({\mathscr{S}}^{n_{1}}_{2}(\theta,\psi))\,p_{2}^{(n_{2})}(\theta,\psi)\,q_{2}({\mathscr{S}}^{n_{2}}_{2}(\theta,\psi))=\left(\prod_{i=0}^{n_{1}-1}p_{3}({\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}(\theta,\psi))\right)q_{3,n_{2}-n_{1}}({\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n_{1}}(\theta,\psi)),\vskip-5.69046pt

with

p_{3}(\theta,\psi)\!:=\!\left(p_{2}(\theta,\psi)\right)^{2}
and
q_{3,n}(\theta,\psi)\!:=\!q_{2}(\theta,\psi)p^{(n)}_{2}(\theta,\psi)\,q_{2}({\mathscr{S}}^{n}_{2}(\theta,\psi))
.
Proposition
4.24
gives

\bigl|p_{2}^{(n_{1})}\!q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}^{n_{1}}_{2}\,p_{2}^{(n_{2})}\!q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}^{n_{2}}_{2}-\langle p_{3}\rangle^{(n_{1})}\langle q_{3,n_{2}-n_{1}}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{1}}\bigr|\leq C\,(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{n_{1}}\rho\,(1+n_{1}\rho)|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|q_{3,n_{2}-n_{1}}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},3}^{-}\,,

where we have
|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|q_{3,n_{2}-n_{1}}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},3}^{-}\leq C\rho^{2}(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{n_{2}-n_{1}}
,
as a consequence of Lemma
4.21
, of the bound (
4.39
) and of the
inequality (
2.6
). Thus we are left with studying

\displaystyle\langle p_{3}\rangle^{(n_{1})}\langle q_{3,n_{2}-n_{1}}\rangle{\circ}\,\overline{G}^{n_{1}}-\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{1})}\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{2})}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\,\overline{G}^{n_{1}}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{2}}

\displaystyle=\langle p_{3}\rangle^{(n_{1})}\bigl(\langle q_{3,n_{2}-n_{1}}\rangle{\circ}\,\overline{G}^{n_{1}}-\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{1}}\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{2}-n_{1})}{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{1}}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{2}}\bigr)

\displaystyle+\bigl(\langle p_{3}\rangle^{(n_{1})}\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{2}-n_{1})}{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{1}}-\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{1})}\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{2})}\bigr)\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{1}}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{2}}.

Using again Proposition
4.24
and (
4.39
), we obtain

\displaystyle\bigl|\langle q_{3,n_{2}-n_{1}}\rangle-\langle q_{2}\rangle\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{2}-n_{1})}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{2}-n_{1}}\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq C\rho^{2}(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{n_{2}-n_{1}}\bigl((1+\alpha_{0}(n_{2}-n_{1}))\lambda^{-\alpha_{0}(n_{2}-n_{1})}+\rho+\rho^{2}(n_{2}-n_{1})\bigr),

while the first bound in (
4.38
) yields
|\langle p_{3}\rangle-\langle p_{2}\rangle^{2}|\leq C\rho^{2}
, which in turn gives

\displaystyle\bigl|\langle p_{3}\rangle^{(n_{1})}\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{2}-n_{1})}{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{1}}-\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{1})}\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{2})}\bigr|=\bigl|\bigl(\langle p_{3}\rangle^{(n_{1})}-\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{1})}\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{1})}\bigr)\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{2}-n_{1})}{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{1}}\bigr|

\displaystyle=\big|\sum_{i=0}^{n_{1}}\langle p_{3}\rangle^{(i)}\bigl(\langle p_{3}\rangle-\langle p_{2}\rangle^{2}\bigr){\circ}\overline{G}^{i}\,\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n_{2}-n_{1})}{\circ}\overline{G}^{n_{1}}\bigr|\leq C\,(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{n_{1}+n_{2}}n_{1}\rho^{2},

Combining all the estimates together and proceeding like in (
4.36
), we get, respectively,

\biggl\langle\biggl(\,\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(p^{(n)}_{2}\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}-\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n)}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n}\right)\biggr)^{\!2}\biggr\rangle\leq C\rho,\qquad\biggl(\,\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(\langle p_{2}\rangle^{(n)}\langle q_{2}\rangle{\circ}\overline{G}^{n}-\overline{p}^{(n)}\,\overline{q}{\circ}\overline{G}^{n}\right)\biggr)^{\!\!2}\leq C\rho.\vskip-5.69046pt

which, together, provides the second bound in (
4.37
).
∎

The following result extends the analysis above to the first derivatives of
h_{2}
and
\overline{h}
;
the proof, based on the same ideas used for Proposition
4.26

up to technical intricacies, is deferred to Appendix
D.1
.

Proposition 4.27
.

​Let
h_{2}
and
\overline{h}
be defined as in (
4.33
) and
(
4.23
), respectively. For all
\theta\!\in\!{\mathcal{U}}
, one has

\bigl|\left\langle\partial_{\theta}h_{2}(\theta,\cdot)-\partial_{\theta}\overline{h}(\theta)\right\rangle\bigr|\leq C\rho,\qquad\left\langle(\partial_{\theta}h_{2}(\theta,\cdot)-\partial_{\theta}\overline{h}(\theta))^{2}\right\rangle\leq C\rho\,.\vskip 2.84544pt

(4.43)

Remark 4.28
.

To prove Proposition
4.27
we need
f\in{\mathscr{B}}_{\alpha_{0},5}
while to prove
Theorems
1
to
4
it would be enough to assume
f\in{\mathscr{B}}_{\alpha_{0},2}
. The full
regularity assumed in our hypotheses is required to prove the forthcoming
Proposition
4.43
(see Remark
4.44
).

4.8.4
Comparison between the translated map and the auxiliary map

We start by introducing some notation. If, for
{\mathfrak{p}}_{1},{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}\in{\mathscr{B}}_{0,3}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
, we define

{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}^{(n)}:=\prod_{i=0}^{n-1}{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{i},\qquad{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}^{(n)}:=\prod_{i=0}^{n-1}{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i},\vskip-5.69046pt

(4.44)

reasoning as in Subsection
4.4
we may write

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}^{(n)}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}^{(n)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}^{(k)}\bigl({\mathfrak{p}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k}\bigr)\,{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1},

(4.45a)

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}^{(n)}{\mathfrak{q}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}^{(n)}\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}

\displaystyle=\bigl({\mathfrak{p}}_{1}^{(n)}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}^{(n)}\bigr)\,{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}+{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}^{(n)}\,\bigl({\mathfrak{q}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}-{\mathfrak{q}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\bigr)\,,

(4.45b)

and, in a similar way, we find

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}^{(n)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{i}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}^{(n)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}^{(k)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{i}\bigl({\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{i}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}\bigr)\,{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1+i}

(4.46a)

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}^{(n)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{i}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}^{(n)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}^{(k)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{i}\bigl({\mathfrak{p}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k+i}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+i}\bigr)\,{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1+i},

(4.46b)

with the latter reducing to (
4.45a
) for
i=0
.

Now, we come back to Proposition
4.16
. To complete the proof
we are left to study the contributions
h_{1}-h_{2}
and
\partial_{\theta}h_{1}-\partial_{\theta}h_{2}
in (
4.34
).
In the light of (
3.15
) and of the the expansion (
4.45a
), we may write

\displaystyle h_{1}-h_{2}=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\bigl(p_{1}^{(n)}\,q_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}-p_{2}^{(n)}q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\bigr)=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}p_{2}^{(n)}\bigl(q_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}-q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\bigr)

(4.47)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\bigl(p_{1}^{(n)}\!-\!p_{2}^{(n)}\bigr)\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}p_{1}^{(k)}\,(p_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}\!-\!p_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k})\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}(q_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}-q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}).

We estimate
h_{1}-h_{2}
by studying the differences that appear in (
4.47
).
First, we prove a series a technical lemmas based on the structure of the difference
f_{1}-f_{2}
,
and come back to (
4.47
) in Lemma
4.39
below.
To start with, we write
\zeta
as in (
4.30a
) and note
that
|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|\zeta|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{0,2}\leq C
,
while, setting
\zeta_{0}=c_{1}(\theta,\psi)\,W(\psi)+c_{2}(\theta,\psi)\,W(\psi)^{2}
,
we have
\langle\zeta_{0}^{2}\rangle\leq C\rho
and

\langle(\partial_{\theta}\zeta_{0})^{2}\rangle\leq C\rho
by (
2.24
) in Theorem
4
.

Remark 4.29
.

A key observation in the argument used below and in the related appendices is the following.
According to (
4.30a
),
the function
\zeta
can be written as sum of three terms. While the last two terms,
which depend linearly or quadratically on
W
, are controlled by Theorem
4
,
the first one depends on
W
through the function
{\mathfrak{f}}
.
One can write
{\mathfrak{f}}
in terms of the difference
W{\circ}A_{0}-W
(and hence linearly in
W
) as in (
4.30e
),
but, in doing so, a factor
\rho
is lost. However, in order to compare
{\mathscr{S}}_{1}
with
{\mathscr{S}}_{2}
,
one deals with sums over
i
of contributions of the form
\Xi_{i}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}(W{\circ}A_{0}^{i+1}-W{\circ}A_{0}^{i})
,
with
\Xi_{i}
more regular than
W

(see for instance the proof of Lemma
4.39
below).
Thus, one can rearrange the sums and obtain summands of the form

(\Xi_{i+1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i-1}-\Xi_{i}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i})W{\circ}A_{0}^{i}
(see Lemmas
4.35
and
4.37
),
where the differences
\Xi_{i+1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i-1}-\Xi_{i}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}

allow to gain a compensating factor
\rho
.

The following result plays a crucial role in the forthcoming discussion. The proof,
based on the idea illustrated in Remark
4.29
, is given in Appendix
D.2.1

(which we refer to for more details).

Lemma 4.30
.

For any
{\mathfrak{p}}
in
{\mathscr{B}}_{\alpha_{0},3}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
one has

{\mathfrak{p}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}-{\mathfrak{p}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}=\Delta_{{\mathfrak{p}},W,n}:=\rho\sum_{i=0}^{n}{\mathfrak{C}}_{{\mathfrak{p}},n,n-i}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}\;W{\circ}A_{0}^{i}+\rho\,{\mathfrak{R}}_{{\mathfrak{p}},n},\vskip-5.69046pt

(4.48)

for suitable functions
{\mathfrak{C}}_{{\mathfrak{p}},n,0},\ldots,{\mathfrak{C}}_{{\mathfrak{p}},n,n}\in{\mathscr{B}}_{\alpha_{0},2}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
and

{\mathfrak{R}}_{{\mathfrak{p}},n}\in{\mathscr{B}}_{0,2}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
such that

\displaystyle|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{C}}_{{\mathfrak{p}},n,k}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2}^{-}\leq C\,(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{k}\,|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|\partial_{\theta}{\mathfrak{p}}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2},\qquad k=1,\ldots,n-1,

(4.49a)

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{n}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{C}}_{{\mathfrak{p}},n,k}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2}^{-}\leq C\,\rho^{-1}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|\partial_{\theta}{\mathfrak{p}}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2},

(4.49b)

\displaystyle\rho\|{\mathfrak{R}}_{{\mathfrak{p}},n}\|_{\infty}+\langle|{\mathfrak{R}}_{{\mathfrak{p}},n}|\rangle\leq C\,|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|\partial_{\theta}{\mathfrak{p}}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2}.

(4.49c)

The two next results are immediate consequences of Lemma
4.30
.

Lemma 4.31
.

For any
{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}\in{\mathscr{B}}_{\alpha_{0},3}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
and any
{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}\in{\mathscr{B}}_{0,3}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
such that

({\mathfrak{p}}_{1}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2})(\theta,\psi)=\rho\,{\mathfrak{c}}_{1}(\theta,\psi)W(\psi)+\rho\,{\mathfrak{c}}_{2}(\theta,\psi)+\rho\,{\mathfrak{c}}_{3}(\theta)\,{\mathfrak{f}}(\psi),\vskip-2.84544pt

(4.50)

with
{\mathfrak{c}}_{1}\in{\mathscr{B}}_{\alpha_{0},2}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
,
{\mathfrak{c}}_{2}\in{\mathscr{B}}_{0,2}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
,
{\mathfrak{c}}_{3}\in C^{1}({\mathcal{U}},\mathds{R})
and
{\mathfrak{f}}
as
in (
4.30e
),
one has

{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}=\Delta_{{\mathfrak{p}}_{1},{\mathfrak{p}}_{2},W,n}+\rho\,{\mathfrak{c}}_{3}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\,{\mathfrak{f}}{\circ}A_{0}^{n},\vskip-8.5359pt

(4.51)

with

\displaystyle\Delta_{{\mathfrak{p}}_{1},{\mathfrak{p}}_{2},W,n}

\displaystyle:=\rho\sum_{i=0}^{n}{\mathfrak{C}}_{{\mathfrak{p}}_{1},{\mathfrak{p}}_{2},n,n-i}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{i}+\rho\,{\mathfrak{R}}_{{\mathfrak{p}}_{1},{\mathfrak{p}}_{2},n}

(4.52a)

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{C}}_{{\mathfrak{p}}_{1},{\mathfrak{p}}_{2},n,k}

\displaystyle:={\mathfrak{C}}_{{\mathfrak{p}}_{2},n,k}+\delta_{k,0}{\mathfrak{c}}_{1},\qquad k=0,\ldots,n,

(4.52b)

\displaystyle{\mathfrak{R}}_{{\mathfrak{p}}_{1},{\mathfrak{p}}_{2},n}

\displaystyle:={\mathfrak{R}}_{{\mathfrak{p}}_{2},n}+{\mathfrak{c}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}+({\mathfrak{c}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}-{\mathfrak{c}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n})\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{n}.\phantom{\sum^{n}}

(4.52c)

Proof.

Write

{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}={\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}+({\mathfrak{p}}_{1}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}){\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}

and
{\mathfrak{c}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}={\mathfrak{c}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}-{\mathfrak{c}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}+{\mathfrak{c}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}
,
and use Lemma
4.30
to deal with the contribution
{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}
.
∎

Corollary 4.32
.

Let
{\mathfrak{p}}_{1},{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}\in{\mathscr{B}}_{\alpha_{0},3}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
be such that
|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|\partial_{\theta}{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2}\leq C\rho

and (
4.50
) holds, with

|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{c}}_{1}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2}^{-}\leq C
,
and

\rho\|{\mathfrak{c}}_{2}\|_{\infty}+\langle|{\mathfrak{c}}_{2}|\rangle\leq C\rho
.
The functions (
4.52
) satisfy the bounds

\sum_{k=0}^{n}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{C}}_{{\mathfrak{p}}_{1},{\mathfrak{p}}_{2},n,k}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2}\leq C,\qquad\qquad\rho\|{\mathfrak{R}}_{{\mathfrak{p}}_{1},{\mathfrak{p}}_{2},n}\|_{\infty}+\langle|{\mathfrak{R}}_{{\mathfrak{p}}_{1},{\mathfrak{p}}_{2},n}|\rangle\leq C\rho.\vskip-2.84544pt

Proof.

The assertion follows immediately from the bounds (
4.49
) and from Theorem
4
.
∎

Remark 4.33
.

Taking either
{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}=p_{1}
and
{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}=p_{2}
or
{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}=q_{1}
and
{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}=q_{2}
,
with
p_{1}
and
q_{1}
as in (
3.14
) and
p_{2}
and
q_{2}
as in (
4.32
),
the hypotheses of Lemma
4.31
are verified, with the functions
{\mathfrak{c}}_{1}
and
{\mathfrak{c}}_{2}
satisfying the
estimates in Corollary
4.32
in both cases. A straightforward computation gives

{\mathfrak{c}}_{3}(\theta)={\mathfrak{a}}_{1}(\theta):=-2\theta^{-1}c_{0}(\theta)

and
{\mathfrak{c}}_{3}(\theta)={\mathfrak{a}}_{2}(\theta):=-\theta^{-2}c_{0}(\theta)
, respectively, so that

p_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\!-\!p_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\!=\!\Delta_{p_{1},p_{2},W,n}+\rho\,{\mathfrak{a}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\,{\mathfrak{f}}{\circ}A_{0}^{n},\qquad q_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\!-\!q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\!=\!\Delta_{q_{1},q_{2},W,n}+\rho\,{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\,{\mathfrak{f}}{\circ}A_{0}^{n},\vskip-2.84544pt

(4.53)

with
{\mathfrak{a}}_{1},{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}\in C^{\infty}({\mathcal{U}},\mathds{R})
,
\|{\mathfrak{a}}_{1}\|_{\infty}\leq C
and
\|{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}\|_{\infty}\leq C
. Moreover, Theorem
4
,
for any
n\geq 0
one has

|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|\Delta_{p_{1},p_{2},W,n}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2}^{-}\leq C\rho,\qquad|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|\Delta_{q_{1},q_{2},W,n}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2}^{-}\leq C\rho.\vskip-2.84544pt

(4.54)

If one restricts
{\mathscr{S}}
to
\Lambda_{2r_{2}}
(see Remark
4.17
),
c_{0}
is replaced with
0
and both functions
{\mathfrak{a}}_{1}
and
{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}
vanish, so that
the terms with
{\mathfrak{c}}_{3}
in (
4.50
) and (
4.51
) disappear.
In particular, the coming Lemmas
4.35
and
4.37

are not needed, and, in the discussion of the remaining results,
all terms involving the functions
{\mathfrak{a}}_{1}
and
{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}
vanish,
with a substantial simplification of the proofs.

Remark 4.34
.

Bounds analogous to (
4.54
) hold for
\Delta_{p_{2},W,n}
and
\Delta_{q_{2},W,n}
. Indeed, for any
n\geq 0
, one has

|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|\Delta_{p_{2},W,n}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2}^{-}\leq C\rho

and

|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|\Delta_{q_{2},W,n}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2}^{-}\leq C\rho
.
Moreover, by Theorem
4
, for any
n,k\geq 0
one has

\displaystyle\big\langle{\bigl|\Delta_{p_{2},W,n}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{k}\bigr|}\big\rangle\leq C\rho^{2},\phantom{\sum}

\displaystyle\qquad\big\langle{\bigl|\Delta_{q_{2},W,n}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{k}\bigr|}\big\rangle\leq C\rho^{2},

(4.55a)

\displaystyle\big\langle{\bigl|\Delta_{p_{1},p_{2},W,n}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{k}\bigr|}\big\rangle\leq C\rho^{2},\qquad

\displaystyle\big\langle{\bigl|\Delta_{p_{1},p_{2},W,n}\,\Delta_{q_{1},q_{2},W,k}\bigr|}\big\rangle\leq C\rho^{3}.

(4.55b)

Corollary
4.32
allows us to deal with the first two contributions in the r.h.s. of (
4.51
).
In order to deal with the last contribution we need also the following two results.

Lemma 4.35
.

For
{\mathfrak{p}}_{1},{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}\in{\mathscr{B}}_{0,3}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
, such that (
4.50
) is satisfied and
\|{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}-1\|_{\infty}\leq C\rho
,
let
{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}^{(n)}
and
{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}^{(n)}
be defined as in (
4.44
).
For any function
{\mathfrak{a}}\!:{\mathcal{U}}\to\mathds{R}
of class
C^{1}
and any
n\geq 1
and

j\geq 0
, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}^{(k)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{j}\,{\mathfrak{a}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k+j}{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}^{(n-1-k)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1+j}\bigl(W{\circ}A_{0}^{k+1+j}-W{\circ}A_{0}^{k+j}\bigr)

(4.56)

\displaystyle=\sum_{k=0}^{n}{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}^{(k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{j}\,{\mathfrak{D}}_{{\mathfrak{a}},n,k,j}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k+j}\,{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}^{(n-1-k)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1+j}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{k+j},

with

{\mathfrak{D}}_{{\mathfrak{a}},n,k,j}:=\begin{cases}-{\mathfrak{a}},&\quad k=0,\\
{\mathfrak{a}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-1}\,{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+j}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-(k+j)}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-1}\,{\mathfrak{a}},&\quad k=1,\ldots,n-1,\\
{\mathfrak{a}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-1},&\quad k=n,\end{cases}

(4.57)

such that

\|{\mathfrak{D}}_{{\mathfrak{a}},n,k,j}\|_{\infty}\leq C\rho\,|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{a}}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{0,1},\qquad k=1,\ldots,n-1.

(4.58)

Proof.

The identity (
4.56
) is easily checked. To bound
{\mathfrak{D}}_{{\mathfrak{a}},n,k,j}
for
k=1,\ldots,n-1
, write

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\mathfrak{a}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-1}\,{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+j}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-(k+j)}-{\mathfrak{a}}\,{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-1}

\displaystyle=\bigl({\mathfrak{a}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-1}-{\mathfrak{a}}\bigr){\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+j}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-(k+j)}+{\mathfrak{a}}\bigl({\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+j}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k+j}\bigr){\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-(k+j)}+{\mathfrak{a}}\bigl({\mathfrak{p}}_{1}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-1}\bigr),

and use that

\displaystyle\bigl\|\bigr({\mathfrak{a}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-1}-{\mathfrak{a}}\bigr){\mathfrak{p}}_{2}\bigr\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\partial_{\theta}{\mathfrak{a}}\|_{\infty}\|({\mathscr{S}}_{1})_{\theta}-\mathds{1}\|_{\infty}\leq C\rho\,\|\partial_{\theta}{\mathfrak{a}}\|_{\infty},

\displaystyle\bigl\|{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+j}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k+j}\bigr\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\rho\sum_{i=0}^{k+j}\|{\mathfrak{C}}_{{\mathfrak{p}}_{1},{\mathfrak{p}}_{2},k+j,k+j-i}\|_{\infty}+\rho\|{\mathfrak{R}}_{{\mathfrak{p}}_{1},{\mathfrak{p}}_{2},k+j}\|_{\infty}+\rho\|{\mathfrak{c}}_{3}\|_{\infty}\|{\mathfrak{f}}\|_{\infty}\leq C\rho,

\displaystyle\bigl\|{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{-1}\bigr\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|(1+O(\rho))-(1+O(\rho))\|_{\infty}\leq C\rho,

with the second inequality following from (
4.51
) in Lemma
4.31
and the bounds in Corollary
4.32
.
∎

Remark 4.36
.

The coefficients
{\mathfrak{D}}_{{\mathfrak{a}},n,k,j}
with
k=0,\ldots,n-1
in (
4.57
) do not depend on
n
, in the sense that

{\mathfrak{D}}_{{\mathfrak{a}},n,k,j}:={\mathfrak{D}}_{{\mathfrak{a}},n^{\prime},k,j}
for all
k<\min\{n,n^{\prime}\}
. Thus, we may define, for future convenience,

{\mathfrak{D}}_{{\mathfrak{a}},k,j}:={\mathfrak{a}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}{\vphantom{s}}^{-1}\,{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}{\vphantom{s}}^{k+j}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}{\vphantom{s}}^{-(k+j)}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}{\vphantom{s}}^{-1}{\mathfrak{a}}.

Lemma 4.37
.

Let
{\mathfrak{p}}_{1},{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}\in{\mathscr{B}}_{0,3}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
be as in Lemma
4.35
, and let

{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}^{(n)}
and
{\mathfrak{p}}^{(n)}_{2}
be defined as in (
4.44
).
For any function
{\mathfrak{a}}\!:{\mathcal{U}}\to\mathds{R}
of class
C^{3}
one has

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}^{(n)}\,{\mathfrak{a}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\bigl(W{\circ}A_{0}^{n+1}-W{\circ}A_{0}^{n}\bigr)=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}^{(n-1)}\,{\mathfrak{D}}_{0,{\mathfrak{a}},n}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{n},

(4.59)

with

{\mathfrak{D}}_{0,{\mathfrak{a}},n}:=\begin{cases}-{\mathfrak{a}},&\quad n=0,\\
{\mathfrak{a}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-1}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-1}\,{\mathfrak{a}},&\quad n\geq 1,\end{cases}

(4.60)

and, similarly, for
r=1,2
,

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}^{(n)}\,{\mathfrak{a}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{r}^{n}\bigl(W{\circ}A_{0}^{n+1}-W{\circ}A_{0}^{n}\bigr)=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}^{(n-1)}\,{\mathfrak{D}}_{r,{\mathfrak{a}},n}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{r}^{n}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{n},\vskip-5.69046pt

(4.61)

with

{\mathfrak{D}}_{r,{\mathfrak{a}},n}:=\begin{cases}-{\mathfrak{a}},&\quad n=0,\\
{\mathfrak{a}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{r}^{-1}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n-1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{r}^{-n}\,{\mathfrak{a}},&\quad n\geq 1.\end{cases}

(4.62)

Furthermore,
if
\rho
is such that
{\mathscr{S}}_{2}
satisfies Hypotheses
1
–
3
,
one has

\displaystyle\|{\mathfrak{D}}_{r,{\mathfrak{a}},n}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C\rho\,|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{a}}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{0,1},\qquad n\geq 1,\quad r=0,1,2,\phantom{\Big(}

(4.63a)

\displaystyle|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{D}}_{2,{\mathfrak{a}},n}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{0,2}

\displaystyle\leq C\rho\,|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{a}}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{0,3},\qquad n\geq 1.\phantom{\Big(}

(4.63b)

Proof.

After checking (
4.59
) and (
4.61
) by direct computation,
the bounds (
4.63a
) are easily obtained by writing

{\mathfrak{a}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-1}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n-1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-n}\,{\mathfrak{a}}={\mathfrak{a}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-1}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-1}\,{\mathfrak{a}}+\left({\mathfrak{p}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n-1}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n-1}\right){\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-n}

in (
4.62
)
and using that

\|{\mathfrak{a}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{r}^{-1}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{r}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{r}^{-1}\,{\mathfrak{a}}\|_{\infty}\|{\mathfrak{a}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{r}^{-1}-{\mathfrak{a}}\|_{\infty}+\|(1-{\mathfrak{p}}_{r}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{r}^{-1})\|_{\infty}\|{\mathfrak{a}}\|_{\infty}\leq C\rho\,|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{a}}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{0,1}

for
r=1,2
,
and

\|{\mathfrak{p}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n-1}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n-1}\|_{\infty}\leq C\rho
,
with the second bound holding by Lemma
4.31
.

The bound (
4.63b
) is obtained by writing

{\mathfrak{a}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{-1}-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{-1}\,{\mathfrak{a}}=\bigl({\mathfrak{a}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{-1}-{\mathfrak{a}}\bigr)+\bigl(1-{\mathfrak{p}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{-1}\bigr){\mathfrak{a}}

and using the bounds of Lemma
4.21
. ∎

Also the next result, essentially based on Proposition
4.24
, is used at length in what follows.

Proposition 4.38
.

Let
{\mathfrak{p}}_{0},\ldots,{\mathfrak{p}}_{k}\in{\mathscr{B}}_{\alpha_{0},3}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
satisfy
the properties 1 and 2 in Lemma
4.21
,
and let
{\mathfrak{p}}^{(k)}(\theta,\psi):={\mathfrak{p}}^{(k)}_{0}({\mathscr{S}}_{2};\theta,\psi)
be defined according to (
4.19
).
For any
{\mathfrak{C}}\in{\mathscr{B}}_{\alpha_{0},2}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
and
{\mathfrak{u}}\in{\mathscr{B}}_{\alpha_{0},3}(\Omega,\mathds{R})
,
and for any
\gamma^{\prime}\in(0,\gamma)
and
i=0,\ldots,k
, one has

\Bigl|\Big\langle{{\mathfrak{p}}^{(k)}{\mathfrak{C}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{i}\,{\mathfrak{u}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}}\Big\rangle\Bigr|\leq C(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{k}\left((1+\alpha_{0}i)\lambda^{-\alpha_{0}i}+\rho+i\rho^{2}+i^{2}\rho^{3}\right)|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{C}}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2}^{-}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{u}}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},3}^{-}.\vskip-2.84544pt

Proof.

For
i=0,\ldots,k
,
let
{\mathfrak{p}}^{(k)}_{[i]}(\theta,\psi):={\mathfrak{p}}^{(k)}_{[i]}({\mathscr{S}}_{2};\theta,\psi)
be as in (
4.19
) with
{\mathscr{S}}={\mathscr{S}}_{2}
.
We have

\displaystyle\hbox{}\hskip 5.69046pt{\mathfrak{p}}^{(k)}{\mathfrak{C}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{i}\,{\mathfrak{u}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}

\displaystyle={\mathfrak{p}}^{(k)}\,{\mathfrak{C}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}\,(W-W_{0}){\circ}A_{0}^{i}\,{\mathfrak{u}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}+{\mathfrak{p}}^{(i)}\,{\mathfrak{p}}^{(k-i)}_{[i]}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}\,{\mathfrak{C}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}\,\mu^{(i)}\,W_{0}\,{\mathfrak{u}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\phantom{\sum^{i}}

\displaystyle+\rho\sum_{j=1}^{i}{\mathfrak{p}}^{(i-j)}\,{\mathfrak{p}}^{(k-i+j)}_{[i-j]}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i-j}\,{\mathfrak{C}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}\,\mu^{(j-1)}{\circ}A_{0}^{i-(j-1)}\,b{\circ}A_{0}^{i-j}\,{\mathfrak{u}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\,,

by (
4.1
) and Remark
4.5
, so that its average can be bounded as

\displaystyle\Bigl|\Big\langle{{\mathfrak{p}}^{(k)}{\mathfrak{C}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{i}\,{\mathfrak{u}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}}\Big\rangle\Bigr|\leq C(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{k}\rho\,\|{\mathfrak{C}}\|_{\infty}\|{\mathfrak{u}}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+C(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{k}\Bigl(\bigl((1+\alpha_{0}i)\lambda^{-\alpha_{0}i}+\rho+i\rho^{2}\bigr))+\rho\sum_{j=1}^{i}\left(\rho+(i-j)\rho^{2}\right)\Bigr)|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{C}}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},3}^{-}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{u}}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},3}^{-}

\displaystyle+C\rho\sum_{j=1}^{i}\Bigl|\langle{\mathfrak{p}}\rangle^{(i-j)}\left\langle b\,{\mathfrak{p}}_{i-j}({\mathfrak{p}}\,\mu)^{(j-1)}_{[i-j+1]}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}\,({\mathfrak{p}}^{(k-i)}_{[i]}\,{\mathfrak{C}}){\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{j}\,{\mathfrak{u}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1-i+j}\right\rangle\Bigr|,

where we have used (
4.14
) to obtain the first line and
applied Proposition
4.24
twice: first with
g_{+}\!=\!W_{0}
,
g_{-}\!=\!{\mathfrak{p}}^{(k-i)}_{[i]}{\mathfrak{C}}\,{\mathfrak{u}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1-i}

and
{\mathfrak{p}}^{(n)}
replaced with
({\mathfrak{p}}\mu)^{(i)}

to obtain the second line; then, after writing

{\mathfrak{p}}_{[i-j]}^{(k-i+j)}\!\!=\!{\mathfrak{p}}_{i-j}{\mathfrak{p}}_{[i-j+1]}^{(j-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}{\mathfrak{p}}_{[i]}^{(k-i)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{j}
,
with
g_{+}\!=\!1
,
g_{-}\!=\!b\,{\mathfrak{p}}_{i-j}({\mathfrak{p}}\,\mu)^{(j-1)}_{[i-j+1]}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}\,({\mathfrak{p}}^{(k-i)}_{[i]}\,{\mathfrak{C}}){\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{j}\,{\mathfrak{u}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1-i+j}
and
{\mathfrak{p}}^{(n)}
replaced with
{\mathfrak{p}}^{(i-j)}
, to obtain the last two lines.

Using once more Proposition
4.24
, with

g_{+}=b
,
g_{-}=({\mathfrak{p}}^{(k-i)}_{[i]}\,{\mathfrak{C}})\,{\mathfrak{u}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1-i}

and
{\mathfrak{p}}^{(n)}
replaced with
{\mathfrak{p}}_{i-j}({\mathfrak{p}}\,\mu)^{(j-1)}_{[i-j+1]}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}
,
in the last line we bound

\displaystyle\Bigl|\Bigl\langle b\,{\mathfrak{p}}_{i-j}({\mathfrak{p}}\,\mu)^{(j-1)}_{[i-j+1]}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}\,({\mathfrak{p}}^{(k-i)}_{[i]}\,{\mathfrak{C}}){\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{j}\,{\mathfrak{u}}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1-i+j}\Bigr\rangle\Bigr|

\displaystyle\leq C(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{k-(i-j)}\left((1+\alpha_{0}j)\lambda^{-\alpha_{0}j}+\rho+j\rho^{2}\right)|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{C}}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},3}^{-}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{u}}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},3}^{-}.

Collecting all the bounds together, we obtain the assertion.
∎

We can now come back to the study of
h_{1}-h_{2}
as represented in
(
4.47
). We study separately the averages of the three contributions
in (
4.47
), starting from the last one.

Lemma 4.39
.

For
p_{1}
and
q_{1}
as in (
3.14
), and

p_{2}
and
q_{2}
as in (
4.32
),
let
p_{1}^{(n)}
and
p_{2}^{(n)}
be defined as in (
3.15
) and in (
4.33
), respectively.
One has

\left|\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\left\langle p_{1}^{(k)}(p_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}\!-\!p_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k})\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}(q_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\!-\!q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n})\right\rangle\right|\leq C\rho.\vskip-2.84544pt

Proof.

Using (
4.30e
) and the notation in (
4.48
) and in (
4.51
), we get

\displaystyle p_{1}^{(k)}(p_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}\!-\!p_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k})\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}(q_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\!-\!q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n})

(4.64)

\displaystyle=p_{1}^{(k)}\Delta_{p_{1},p_{2},W,k}\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\Delta_{q_{1},q_{2},W,n}

\displaystyle+p_{1}^{(k)}\,{\mathfrak{a}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}\,(W{\circ}A_{0}^{k+1}-W{\circ}A_{0}^{k})\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\Delta_{q_{1},q_{2},W,n}

\displaystyle+p_{1}^{(k)}\Delta_{p_{1},p_{2},W,k}\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\,{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\,(W{\circ}A_{0}^{n+1}-W{\circ}A_{0}^{n})

\displaystyle+p_{1}^{(k)}\,{\mathfrak{a}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}\,(W{\circ}A_{0}^{k+1}-W{\circ}A_{0}^{k})\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\,{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\,(W{\circ}A_{0}^{n+1}-W{\circ}A_{0}^{n}).

The bounds (
4.55
) give immediately

\left|\left\langle p_{1}^{(k)}\Delta_{p_{1},p_{2},W,k}\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\Delta_{q_{1},q_{2},W,n}\right\rangle\right|\leq C\rho^{3}\,n\,(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{n}.\vskip-2.84544pt

Furthermore, by applying Lemma
4.35
using the bounds (
4.55
) and (
4.58
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\left\langle p_{1}^{(k)}\,{\mathfrak{a}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}\,(W{\circ}A_{0}^{k+1}-W{\circ}A_{0}^{k})\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\Delta_{q_{1},q_{2},W,n}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{k=0}^{n}\left\langle p_{1}^{(k-1)}\,{\mathfrak{D}}_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{1},n,k,0}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{k}\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\,\Delta_{q_{1},q_{2},W,n}\right\rangle\right|\leq C\rho^{2}\,n\,(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{n}.

Analogously, using Lemma
4.37
and the expansion (
4.46a
) together with the notation in (
4.51
), to write

\displaystyle\biggl|\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\left\langle p_{1}^{(k)}\Delta_{p_{1},p_{2},W,k}\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\,{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\,(W{\circ}A_{0}^{n+1}-W{\circ}A_{0}^{n})\right\rangle\biggr|

\displaystyle=\biggl|\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\Bigl\langle p_{1}^{(k)}\Delta_{p_{1},p_{2},W,k}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\bigl(p_{2}^{(n-1)}{\mathfrak{D}}_{1,{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},n}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}W{\circ}A_{0}^{n}\bigr){\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k+1}\Bigr\rangle-\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\Bigl\langle p_{1}^{(k)}\Delta_{p_{1},p_{2},W,k}\Bigr\rangle

\displaystyle-\!\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\Bigl\langle p_{1}^{(k)}\!\Delta_{p_{1},p_{2},W,k}\!\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}p_{2}^{(i)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k+1}\Delta_{p_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i},W,k+1}p_{2}^{(n-1-i)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+2+i}\rho{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n+k+1}{\mathfrak{f}}{\circ}A_{0}^{n+k+1}\Bigr\rangle\biggr|\leq C\rho,

where we have used (
4.55
) and (
4.63a
).
Finally, using Lemma
4.35
and Remark
4.36
,
hence Lemma
4.37
and thence the expansions (
4.46
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\Bigl\langle p_{1}^{(k)}\,{\mathfrak{a}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}\,(W{\circ}A_{0}^{k+1}-W{\circ}A_{0}^{k})\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\,{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\,(W{\circ}A_{0}^{n+1}-W{\circ}A_{0}^{n})\Bigr\rangle

\displaystyle=\!\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\Bigl\langle p_{1}^{(k-1)}\,{\mathfrak{D}}_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{1},k,0}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{k}\,\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\bigl(p_{2}^{(n-1)}\,{\mathfrak{D}}_{1,{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},n}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{n}\bigr){\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k+1}\Bigr\rangle

\displaystyle-\!\!\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\Bigl\langle p_{1}^{(k-1)}{\mathfrak{D}}_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{1},k,0}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}W{\circ}A_{0}^{k}\!\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\!\sum_{i=0}^{n-1}p_{2}^{(i)}\!{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k+1}\!\Delta_{p_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i},W,k+1}p_{2}^{(n-1-i)}\!{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+2+i}\!\rho{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n+k+1}{\mathfrak{f}}{\circ}A_{0}^{n+k+1}\Bigr\rangle

\displaystyle+\!\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Bigl\langle p_{1}^{(n-1)}\,\Delta_{1,{\mathfrak{a}}_{1}{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1},n}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\bigl(W{\circ}A_{0}^{n+1}\bigr)^{2}\Bigr\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\Bigl\langle p_{1}^{(n)}\,\big({\mathfrak{a}}_{1}\,{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}\bigr){\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\bigl(\rho\,{\mathfrak{f}}{\circ}A_{0}^{n+1}\bigr)^{2}\Bigr\rangle,

where the two contributions in the last line have been obtained writing

\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}p_{1}^{(n-1)}\,{\mathfrak{a}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n-1}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{n}\rho\,{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}{\mathfrak{f}}{\circ}A_{0}^{n},

(4.65)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}p_{1}^{(n)}\,\big({\mathfrak{a}}_{1}\,{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}\bigr){\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\Bigl((W{\circ}A_{0}^{n+2})^{2}-(W{\circ}A_{0}^{n+1})^{2}-\bigl(\rho\,{\mathfrak{f}}{\circ}A_{0}^{n+1}\bigr)^{2}\Bigr)

and then proceeding as in the proof of Lemma
4.35
.
Thus, relying once more on (
4.55
), (
4.58
) and (
4.63a
), we get the assertion.
∎

The first application of Proposition
4.38
is to estimate the other two contributions in (
4.47
).
This leads to the two following lemmas, whose proof makes also use of the
argument given in Remark
4.29

(in particular of Lemmas
4.30
,
4.35
and
4.37
, which
are based on the latter).

Lemma 4.40
.

Let
p_{2}^{(n)}
be defined as in (
4.33
), with
q_{1}
as in (
3.14
) and
p_{2},q_{2}
as in (
4.32
).
One has

\biggl|\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left\langle p_{2}^{(n)}\bigl(q_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}-q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\bigr)\right\rangle\biggr|\leq C\rho.\vskip-5.69046pt

Proof.

Using Lemma
4.31
and Remark
4.33
, we write

\displaystyle\hbox{}\hskip-8.5359pt\biggl|\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left\langle p_{2}^{(n)}\left(q_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}-q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\right)\right\rangle\biggr|\leq\biggl|\rho\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{n}\left\langle p_{2}^{(n)}{\mathfrak{C}}_{q_{1},q_{2},n,n-k}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{k}\right\rangle\biggr|\!+\!\biggl|\rho\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left\langle p_{2}^{(n)}R_{q_{1},q_{2},n}\right\rangle\biggr|

(4.66)

\displaystyle+\biggl|\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left\langle p_{2}^{(n)}{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\bigl(W{\circ}A_{0}^{n+1}-W{\circ}A_{0}^{n}\bigr)\right\rangle\biggr|+\biggl|\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left\langle p_{2}^{(n)}\,\rho\bigl({\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}-{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\bigr){\mathfrak{f}}{\circ}A_{0}^{n}\right\rangle\biggr|.

We use Proposition
4.38
, with
k
and
i
replaced with
n
and
k
, respectively,

{\mathfrak{p}}_{j}=p_{2}

\forall j=1,\ldots,k-1
,

{\mathfrak{C}}={\mathfrak{C}}_{q_{1},q_{2},n,n-k}
and
{\mathfrak{u}}=1
, and the estimates (
4.49
)
in Lemma
4.30
to bound the second line in (
4.66
).
In the first contribution of the third line, using Lemma
4.37
,
we write, for
n\geq 1
,

\biggl|\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left\langle p_{2}^{(n)}{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\bigl(W{\circ}A_{0}^{n+1}-W{\circ}A_{0}^{n}\bigr)\right\rangle\biggr|=\biggl|\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left\langle p_{2}^{(n-1)}{\mathfrak{D}}_{2,{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},n}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{n}\right\rangle\biggr|\\
\leq C\rho,\vskip-2.84544pt

(4.67)

where the last bound follows from Proposition
4.38
, with
k=n-1
,
i=n
,
{\mathfrak{p}}_{j}=p_{2}
for
j=0,\ldots,n-2

(see Remark
4.23
),
{\mathfrak{C}}={\mathfrak{D}}_{2,{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},n}
,
so that
|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{C}}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2}^{-}=|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{D}}_{2,{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},n}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2}^{-}\leq C\rho
for
n\geq 1
,
and
{\mathfrak{u}}=1
. To deal with the second contribution in the third line of (
4.66
), setting

{\mathfrak{f}}_{0}(\psi):=f(0,\psi),\qquad{\mathfrak{f}}{\circ}A_{0}^{n}-{\mathfrak{f}}_{0}{\circ}A_{0}^{n}={\mathfrak{g}}_{n}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{n},\qquad{\mathfrak{g}}_{n}(\psi):=\int_{0}^{1}\,\partial_{\theta}{\mathfrak{f}}(tW(A_{0}^{n}\psi),A_{0}\psi),\vskip-5.69046pt

(4.68)

and using Lemma
4.30
, we rewrite

\bigl({\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}-{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\bigr){\mathfrak{f}}{\circ}A_{0}^{n}=\biggl(\rho\sum_{k=0}^{n}{\mathfrak{C}}_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},n,n-k}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{k}+\rho R_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},n}\biggr)\bigl({\mathfrak{f}}_{0}{\circ}A_{0}^{n}+{\mathfrak{g}}_{n}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{n}\bigr),\vskip-2.84544pt

so that, by exploiting Proposition
4.38
, with
k
and
i
replaced with
n
and
k
, respectively, and with
{\mathfrak{u}}={\mathfrak{f}}_{0}{\circ}A_{0}^{-1}
,
Theorem
4
and the bounds of Lemma
4.30
, we obtain,
for any
\gamma^{\prime}\in(0,\gamma)

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{n}\rho^{2}\left\langle p_{2}^{(n)}{\mathfrak{C}}_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},n,n-k}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{k}\,{\mathfrak{f}}_{0}{\circ}A_{0}^{n}\right\rangle\right|=\left|\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\rho^{2}\left\langle p_{2}^{(n+k)}{\mathfrak{C}}_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},n+k,n}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{k}\,{\mathfrak{f}}_{0}{\circ}A_{0}^{n+k}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\rho^{2}(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{k}\bigl((1+\alpha_{0}k)\,\lambda^{-\alpha_{0}k}+\rho+k\rho^{2}+k\rho^{3}\bigr)\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\mathfrak{C}}_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},n+k,n}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},2}^{-}\leq C\rho,

and

\displaystyle\biggl|\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\rho^{2}\left\langle p_{2}^{(n)}\,R_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},n}\,{\mathfrak{f}}_{0}{\circ}A_{0}^{n}\right\rangle\biggr|\leq C\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\rho^{2}(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{n}\bigl\langle\bigl|{\mathfrak{R}}_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},n}\bigr|\bigr\rangle\leq C\rho,

\displaystyle\biggl|\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\rho^{2}\biggl\langle p_{2}^{(n)}{\mathfrak{g}}_{n}\biggl(\sum_{k=0}^{n}{\mathfrak{C}}_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},n,n-k}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{k}+R_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},n}\biggr)W{\circ}A_{0}^{n}\biggr\rangle\biggr|\leq C\rho,

which imply the desired bound.
∎

Lemma 4.41
.

Let
p_{1}^{(n)}
and
p_{2}^{(n)}
be defined as in (
3.15
) and in (
4.33
), respectively, with
p_{1}
and
q_{1}
as in (
3.14
), and

p_{2}
and
q_{2}
as in (
4.32
). One has

\biggl|\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left\langle\bigl(p_{1}^{(n)}-p_{2}^{(n)}\bigr)\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\right\rangle\biggr|\leq C\rho.\vskip-5.69046pt

Proof.

We expand
p_{1}^{(n)}-p_{2}^{(n)}
as in (
4.45a
), hence split

p_{1}^{(k)}=p_{2}^{(k)}+(p_{1}^{(k)}-p_{2}^{(k)})
and thence expand again
p_{1}^{(k)}-p_{2}^{(k)}
, so as to obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left\langle\bigl(p_{1}^{(n)}\!-\!p_{2}^{(n)}\bigr)\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\right\rangle=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}p_{2}^{(k)}\left\langle\bigl(p_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}-p_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k}\bigr)p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\right\rangle

(4.69)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}\left\langle p_{1}^{(j)}\bigl(p_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{j}\!-\!p_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{j}\bigr)p_{2}^{(k-1-j)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{j+1}\,\bigl(p_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}\!-\!p_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k}\bigr)p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\right\rangle,

To bound the second line of (
4.69
), we write

p_{1}^{(j)}\bigl(p_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{j}-p_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{j}\bigr)p_{2}^{(k-1-j)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{j+1}\,\bigl(p_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}-p_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k}\bigr)

as in (
4.64
), with
k
,
j
,
p_{1}
and
p_{2}
instead of
n
,
k
,
q_{1}
and
q_{2}
, respectively,
and we reason as in the proof of Lemma
4.39
,
by relying on Lemmas
4.35
and
4.37
, and using
(
4.65
). Thus, the second line becomes

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}\left\langle p_{1}^{(j)}\Delta_{p_{1},p_{2},W,j}\,p_{2}^{(k-1-j)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{j+1}\,\Delta_{p_{1},p_{2},W,k}\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}\left\langle p_{1}^{(j-1)}\,{\mathfrak{D}}_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{1},j,0}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{j}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{j}\,p_{2}^{(k-j-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{j+1}\,\Delta_{p_{1},p_{2},W,k}\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\left\langle p_{1}^{(k-1)}\,{\mathfrak{a}}_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k-1}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{k}\,\Delta_{p_{1},p_{2},W,k}\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Bigl\langle p_{1}^{(j)}\,\Delta_{p_{1},p_{2},W,j}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\bigl(p_{2}^{(k-1)}\,{\mathfrak{D}}_{1,{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},k}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{k}\bigr){\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{j+1}{\mathcal{Q}}_{k+1}\Bigr\rangle

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Bigl\langle p_{1}^{(j)}\,\Delta_{p_{1},p_{2},W,j}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{i=0}^{k}p_{2}^{(i)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{j+1}\Delta_{p_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i},W,j+1}{\mathcal{P}}_{j,k,i}({\mathfrak{a}}_{2}\rho\,{\mathfrak{f}}){\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k+j+1}{\mathcal{Q}}_{k+1}\Bigr\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Bigl\langle p_{1}^{(j-1)}\,{\mathfrak{D}}_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{1},j,0}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{j}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{j}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\bigl(p_{2}^{(k-1)}{\mathfrak{D}}_{1,{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},k}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{k}\bigr){\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{j+1}{\mathcal{Q}}_{k+1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{j+1}\Bigr\rangle

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\Bigl\langle p_{1}^{(j-1)}\,{\mathfrak{D}}_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{1},j,0}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{j}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{j}\,\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{i=0}^{k}p_{2}^{(i)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{j+1}\Delta_{p_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i},W,j+1}{\mathcal{P}}_{j,k,i}({\mathfrak{a}}_{2}\rho\,{\mathfrak{f}}){\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k+j+1}{\mathcal{Q}}_{k+1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{j+1}\Bigr\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\Bigl\langle p_{1}^{(k-1)}{\mathfrak{D}}_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{1}{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-1},k,0}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}\bigl(W{\circ}A_{0}^{k+1}\bigr)^{\!2}{\mathcal{Q}}_{k+1}\Bigr\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\Bigl\langle p_{1}^{(k)}\!\bigl({\mathfrak{a}}_{1}{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{-1}\bigr){\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}\bigl(\rho\,{\mathfrak{f}}{\circ}A_{0}^{k+1}\bigr)^{\!2}{\mathcal{Q}}_{k+1}\Bigr\rangle,

with
{\mathcal{Q}}_{k}:=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\bigl(p_{2}^{(n)}\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\bigr){\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k}

and
{\mathcal{P}}_{j,k,i}:=p_{2}^{(k-1-i)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{j+i+2}
,
so that all contributions are found to be bounded as
C\rho
.

The contribution in the first line of the r.h.s. of (
4.69
) is dealt with by writing
p_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}-p_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k}

once more according to Remark
4.33
, so that we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\left\langle p_{2}^{(k)}\left(p_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}-p_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k}\right)p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\right\rangle

(4.70)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\biggl\langle p_{2}^{(k)}\biggl(\rho\sum_{i=0}^{k}{\mathfrak{C}}_{p_{1},p_{2},k,k-i}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{i}+\rho\,{\mathfrak{R}}_{p_{1},p_{2},k}\biggr)p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\biggr\rangle

\displaystyle+\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{n}\left\langle p_{2}^{(k)}\,\rho\,{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}\,{\mathfrak{f}}{\circ}A_{0}^{k}\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\right\rangle.

In (
4.70
) the second line
is bounded by Proposition
4.38
, with
{\mathfrak{p}}_{i}=p_{2}

\forall i=1,\ldots,k-1
,

{\mathfrak{C}}={\mathfrak{C}}_{p_{1},p_{2},k,k-i}
and
{\mathfrak{u}}=p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}^{n-k-1}
,
while in order to bound the third line we proceed as follows. After splitting

{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}={\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k}+({\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}-{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k})
,
we reason as done for the last line of (
4.66
):
the contribution with
{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k}
is dealt with as (
4.67
),
the only difference being that, when applying Proposition
4.38
,
one sets
{\mathfrak{u}}=p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n-k}
;
in the second contribution we expand
{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k}-{\mathfrak{a}}_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k}
as in (
4.48
)
and write
{\mathfrak{f}}{\circ}A_{0}^{k}
as in (
4.68
), so as to obtain the three contributions

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left\langle\rho\,p_{2}^{(k+i)}\,\rho\,{\mathfrak{C}}_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},k+i,k}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{i}\,{\mathfrak{f}}_{0}{\circ}A_{0}^{k+1}\,p_{2}^{(n-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+i+1}\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n+k+i}\right\rangle,

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{n}\left\langle\rho\,p_{2}^{(k)}\,\rho\,{\mathfrak{R}}_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},k}\,{\mathfrak{f}}_{0}{\circ}A_{0}^{k+1}\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n}\right\rangle,

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\sum_{k=0}^{n}\biggl\langle\rho\,p_{2}^{(k)}\biggl(\rho\sum_{i=0}^{k}{\mathfrak{C}}_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},k,k-i}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{i}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{i}+\rho\,{\mathfrak{R}}_{{\mathfrak{a}}_{2},k}\biggr){\mathfrak{g}}_{k}\,W{\circ}A_{0}^{k}\,p_{2}^{(n-k-1)}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{k+1}\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{ni}\biggr\rangle,

which are all bounded proportionally to
\rho
.
∎

The following result puts together the bounds obtained above and extends the analysis to the square of
h_{1}-h_{2}
.
Together with the forthcoming Proposition
4.43
, it completes the first step in order to prove Proposition
4.27
,
as outlined at the beginning of the present subsection.

Proposition 4.42
.

Let
h_{2}
and
h_{1}
be as in (
4.33
) and in (
3.11
),
respectively. For all
\theta\!\in\!{\mathcal{U}}
one has

|\left\langle h_{1}(\theta,\cdot)-h_{2}(\theta,\cdot)\right\rangle|\leq C\rho,\qquad\left\langle(h_{1}(\theta,\cdot)-h_{2}(\theta,\cdot))^{2}\right\rangle\leq C\rho\,.

(4.71)

Proof.

The first bound in (
4.71
) follows immediately from (
4.47
)
and from the estimates in Lemma
4.39
,
4.40
and
4.41
.
To obtain the second bound in (
4.71
), we expand
(h_{1}-h_{2})^{2}
as

\displaystyle\sum_{n_{1},n_{2}=0}^{\infty}\!\!\Bigl(p_{1}^{(n_{1})}\!\bigl(q_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n_{1}}\!\!-\!q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n_{1}}\bigr)p_{1}^{(n_{2})}\!\bigl(q_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n_{2}}\!\!-\!q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n_{2}}\bigr)\!+\!p_{1}^{(n_{1})}\!\bigl(q_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n_{1}}\!\!-q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n_{1}}\bigr)\!\bigl(p_{1}^{(n_{2})}\!\!-\!p_{2}^{(n_{2})}\bigr)q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n_{2}}

\displaystyle+\bigl(p_{1}^{(n_{1})}-p_{2}^{(n_{1})}\bigr)\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n_{1}}\,p_{1}^{(n_{2})}\,\bigl(q_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n_{2}}\!-\!q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n_{2}}\bigr)+\bigl(p_{1}^{(n_{1})}\!-\!p_{2}^{(n_{1})}\bigr)\,q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n_{1}}\,\bigl(p_{1}^{(n_{2})}\!-\!p_{2}^{(n_{2})}\bigr)q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n_{2}}\Bigr),

where, writing, for
i=1,2
,

q_{1}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n_{i}}-q_{2}{\circ}{\mathscr{S}}_{2}^{n_{i}}
according to (
4.53
)
and
p_{1}^{(n_{i})}-p_{2}^{(n_{i})}

using first (
4.45a
) and then once more (
4.53
),
we obtain a sum of contributions
which can be dealt with as the contributions in (
4.64
).
Then, using also that
|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|q_{2}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},3}\leq C\rho
, the second bound follows.
∎

Finally, the following results shows that bounds analogous to those of
Proposition
4.42
extend to
the derivatives of the two functions
h_{1}
and
h_{2}
. The proof, which follows the same lines of the proof
of Proposition
4.42
, is given in Appendix
D.2.2
.

Proposition 4.43
.

Let
h_{1}
and
h_{2}
be as in (
3.11
) and in (
4.33
),
respectively. For all
\theta\in{\mathcal{U}}
, one has

|\left\langle\partial_{\theta}h_{1}(\theta,\cdot)-\partial_{\theta}h_{2}(\theta,\cdot)\right\rangle|\leq C\rho,\qquad\left\langle(\partial_{\theta}h_{1}(\theta,\cdot)-\partial_{\theta}h_{2}(\theta,\cdot))^{2}\right\rangle\leq C\rho\,.\vskip 2.84544pt

(4.72)

Remark 4.44
.

By looking at the proof of Proposition
4.43
, we see that
F=\rho f
has to be required to belong to
{\mathscr{B}}_{\alpha_{0},5}
,
because we need to apply Proposition
4.38
.
The further condition that
F
be in
{\mathscr{B}}_{\alpha_{0},6}
is needed in order to control the deviations of the
second derivative of the conjugation, which in turn is used to estimate the deviations of the
first derivative of the inverse conjugation (see Remark
4.46
).

4.8.5
Deviations of the conjugation: proof of the bounds (
2.28a
) and (
2.28b
)

We now come back to
h(\varphi,\psi)-\overline{h}(\varphi)
, with
h(\varphi,\psi)
written as in (
4.27
)
(taking in mind Remark
4.15
for the notation), and estimate the whole average.
After rewriting

\langle h(\varphi,\cdot)-\overline{h}(\varphi)\rangle=\big\langle{h_{1}(\varphi,\cdot)-\overline{h}(\varphi)}\big\rangle+\big\langle{W(\cdot)\,\partial_{\varphi}h_{1}(\varphi,\cdot)}\big\rangle+\!\int_{0}^{1}\!\!{\rm d}t\,(1-t)\,\big\langle{(W(\cdot))^{2}\,\partial_{\varphi}^{2}h_{1}(\varphi+tW(\cdot),\cdot)}\big\rangle,\hskip-5.69046pt

we bound
|\langle h_{1}(\varphi,\cdot)-\overline{h}(\varphi)\rangle|\leq C\rho

by Proposition
4.16
, and

\langle(W(\cdot))^{2}\rangle\leq C\rho
by Theorem
4
. Writing

\big\langle{W(\cdot)\,\partial_{\varphi}h_{1}(\varphi,\cdot)}\big\rangle=\left\langle W(\cdot)\,\partial_{\varphi}(h_{1}(\varphi,\cdot)-\overline{h}(\varphi))\right\rangle+\langle W\rangle\partial_{\varphi}\overline{h}(\varphi)
,
Theorem
4
gives
|\langle W\rangle\partial_{\varphi}\overline{h}(\varphi)|\leq C\rho
while we
use first the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, then
Proposition
4.16
, together again with Theorem
4
, to obtain

|\big\langle{W(\cdot)\,\partial_{\varphi}(h_{1}(\varphi,\cdot)-\overline{h}(\varphi))}\big\rangle|\leq(\big\langle{(W(\cdot))^{2}}\big\rangle\langle(\partial_{\varphi}h_{1}(\varphi,\cdot)-\partial_{\varphi}\overline{h}(\varphi))^{2}\rangle)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C\rho
.
This concludes the proof of the first bound in (
2.28a
).

The second bound in (
2.28a
) is easily obtained by writing

\displaystyle\bigl(h(\varphi,\psi)-\overline{h}(\varphi)\bigr)^{2}

\displaystyle=\biggl(h_{1}(\varphi,\psi)-\overline{h}(\varphi)-W(\psi)\int_{0}^{1}{\rm d}t\,\partial_{\varphi}h(\varphi-tW(\psi),\psi)\biggr)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\left(h_{1}(\varphi,\psi)-\overline{h}(\varphi)\right)^{2}+(W(\psi))^{2}\left\|\partial_{\varphi}h\right\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 4\left(h_{1}(\varphi,\psi)-h_{2}(\varphi,\psi)\right)^{2}+4\left(h_{2}(\varphi,\psi)-\overline{h}(\varphi)\right)^{2}+2(W(\psi))^{2}\left\|\partial_{\varphi}h\right\|^{2},\phantom{\biggr)^{2}}

which in turn is bounded using once more Proposition
4.16
and Theorem
4
.

Now we pass to the bounds (
2.28
).
In order to study the average of
\partial_{\varphi}h(\varphi,\psi)-\partial_{\varphi}\overline{h}(\varphi)
, we write

\displaystyle\partial_{\varphi}h(\varphi,\psi)

\displaystyle=\partial_{\varphi}h_{1}(\varphi,\psi)-W(\psi)\int_{0}^{1}{\rm d}t\,\partial_{\varphi}^{2}h(\varphi-tW(\psi),\psi)=\partial_{\varphi}h_{1}(\varphi,\psi)

(4.73)

\displaystyle-W(\psi)\left(\partial_{\varphi}^{2}h_{1}(\varphi,\psi)\!-\!\partial_{\varphi}^{2}\overline{h}(\varphi)\right)-W(\psi)\,\partial_{\varphi}^{2}\overline{h}(\varphi)+(W(\psi))^{2}\!\!\int_{0}^{1}\!\!{\rm d}t\,\partial_{\varphi}^{3}h(\varphi-tW(\psi),\psi).

Therefore, using the second expansion in (
4.73
), we can bound

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\partial_{\varphi}h(\varphi,\cdot)-\partial_{\varphi}\overline{h}(\varphi)\right\rangle\right|\leq\left|\left\langle\partial_{\varphi}h_{1}(\varphi,\cdot)-\partial_{\varphi}\overline{h}(\varphi)\right\rangle\right|

(4.74)

\displaystyle+\left|\left\langle W(\cdot)\left(\partial_{\varphi}^{2}h_{1}(\varphi,\cdot)-\partial_{\varphi}^{2}\overline{h}(\varphi)\right)\right\rangle\right|+\left\langle W(\psi)\right\rangle\partial_{\varphi}^{2}\overline{h}(\varphi)+\left\langle(W(\psi))^{2}\right\rangle\|\partial_{\varphi}^{3}h\|_{\infty}.

The term in the first line and the last two terms in the second line are bounded by (
4.28b
) and by Theorem
4
, respectively.
To bound the first term in the second line of (
4.74
)
we need the following result, which is proved in Appendix
D.3
.

Lemma 4.45
.

Let
h_{1}
and
\overline{h}
be defined
as in (
3.11
) and in (
4.23
), respectively.
For all
\theta\in{\mathcal{U}}
one has

\bigl|\left\langle(\partial_{\theta}^{2}h_{1}(\theta,\cdot)-\partial_{\theta}^{2}\overline{h}(\theta))^{2}\right\rangle\bigr|\leq C\rho.

Remark 4.46
.

As mentioned in Remark
4.44
, the
condition
F\in{\mathscr{B}}_{\alpha_{0},6}
is required to obtain the bounds in Lemma

4.45
.
A bound like
|\left\langle\partial_{\theta}^{2}h_{1}(\theta,\cdot)-\partial_{\theta}^{2}\overline{h}(\theta)\right\rangle|\leq C\rho

could be obtained as well, but actually we need only the bound on the squared deviations because
the latter will be needed in order to estimate the deviations of first derivative of the inverse conjugation.

By the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and Lemma
4.45
, we get

\left|\left\langle W(\cdot)\left(\partial_{\varphi}^{2}h_{1}(\varphi,\cdot)-\partial_{\varphi}^{2}\overline{h}(\varphi)\right)\right\rangle\right|\leq C\rho
,
which inserted into (
4.74
) yields the first bound in (
2.28b
).

Finally, we pass to the second bound in (
2.28b
).
By using the first expansion in (
4.73
), we may bound

|\partial_{\varphi}h(\varphi,\psi)-\partial_{\varphi}\overline{h}(\varphi)\leq|\partial_{\varphi}h_{1}(\varphi,\psi)-\partial_{\varphi}\overline{h}(\varphi)|+|W(\psi)|\,\|\partial_{\varphi}^{2}h\|_{\infty}
,
which allows us to estimate

\langle(\partial_{\varphi}h(\varphi,\cdot)-\partial_{\varphi}\overline{h}(\varphi))^{2}\rangle\leq 2\langle(\partial_{\varphi}h_{1}(\varphi,\cdot)-\partial_{\varphi}\overline{h}(\varphi))^{2}\rangle+2\|\partial_{\varphi}^{2}h\|_{\infty}^{2}\langle(W(\cdot))^{2}\rangle
.
This immediately implies the second bound in (
2.28b
) by
(
4.43
) and by Theorem
4
.

4.9
Deviations of the inverse conjugation

To deal with the inverse conjugation, we exploit the following trivial identity.

{\mathcal{H}}_{1}({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi),\psi)=\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta))=\eta,

(4.75)

which holds for all
(\eta,\psi)\in\Omega_{0}={\mathcal{U}}_{0}\times\mathds{T}^{2}:=\overline{{\mathscr{H}}}(\Omega)
,
provided
\Omega_{\rm ext}
is such that

{\mathscr{H}}(\Omega_{\rm ext})\supset\overline{{\mathscr{H}}}(\Omega)
and

\Omega_{1,\rm ext}\supset\Omega

(recall that we are working with the extended maps).

Lemma 4.47
.

Let
l_{1}
and
\overline{l}
be defined as in (
3.12
) and in (
2.21
), respectively.
For all
\eta\in{\mathcal{U}}_{0}
, one has

|\left\langle l_{1}(\eta,\cdot)-\overline{l}(\eta)\right\rangle|\leq C\rho,\qquad\left\langle(l_{1}(\eta,\cdot)-\overline{l}(\eta))^{2}\right\rangle\leq C\rho\,.

(4.76)

Proof.

By using (
4.75
) we get

{\mathcal{H}}_{1}({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi),\psi)-{\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta),\psi)=\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta))-{\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta),\psi).\vskip-2.84544pt

(4.77)

If we write the l.h.s. in (
4.77
) as

\bigl({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi)-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)\bigr)\int_{0}^{1}{\rm d}t\,\partial_{\theta}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)+t({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi)-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)),\psi)\vskip-2.84544pt

and use the lower bound (
3.27
), we obtain from (
4.77
)

\gamma_{1}^{2}\big\langle{\bigl({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}\bigr)^{2}}\big\rangle(\eta)\leq\big\langle{\left({\mathcal{H}}_{1}-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}\right)^{2}}\big\rangle\bigl(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)\bigr).\ \vskip-2.84544pt

(4.78)

Analogously, if we write l.h.s. of (
4.77
) as

\displaystyle\bigl({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi)-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)\bigr)\partial_{\theta}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta))+\bigl({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi)-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)\bigr)\left(\partial_{\theta}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta),\psi)-\partial_{\theta}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta))\right)

\displaystyle+\bigl({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi)-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)\bigr)^{2}\int_{0}^{1}(1-t)\,{\rm d}t\,\partial_{\theta}^{2}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)+t({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi)-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)),\psi),

then we find

\displaystyle\overline{\gamma}\bigl|\big\langle{{\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}}\big\rangle(\eta)\bigr|\leq\partial_{\theta}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta))\bigl|\big\langle{{\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}}\big\rangle(\eta)\bigr|\leq\bigl|\big\langle{{\mathcal{H}}_{1}-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}}\big\rangle\bigl(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)\bigr)\bigr|

(4.79)

\displaystyle+\|\partial^{2}_{\theta}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}\|_{\infty}\big\langle{\bigl({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}\bigr)^{2}}\big\rangle(\eta)+\Bigl(\big\langle{\bigl({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}\bigr)^{2}}\big\rangle(\eta)\big\langle{\bigl(\partial_{\theta}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}-\partial_{\theta}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}\bigr)^{2}}\big\rangle\bigl(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)\bigr)\Bigr)^{1/2},

with
\overline{\gamma}=O(1)
as in Remark
3.12
. Thus, using that

{\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\theta,\psi)-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)=\theta^{2}\bigl(h_{1}(\theta,\psi)-\overline{h}(\theta)\bigr)
and
{\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi)-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}{\vphantom{{\mathcal{H}}}}^{-1}(\eta)=\eta^{2}\bigl(l_{1}(\eta,\psi)-\overline{l}(\eta)\bigr)

and that, for
\theta=\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}{\vphantom{{\mathcal{H}}}}^{-1}(\eta)
, there exists a constant
d_{0}
such that
d_{0}^{-1}\leq\theta/\eta\leq d_{0}

(see Section
3.2.4
), the bound (
4.78
), together with the second bound in (
4.28a
),
gives the second bound in (
4.76
),
which, in turn, inserted into (
4.79
), together with the first bound in (
4.28a
) and the second bound in (
4.28b
),
yields the first bound in (
4.76
).
∎

Lemma 4.48
.

Let
l_{1}
and
\overline{l}
be defined as in (
3.12
) and in (
2.21
), respectively.
For all
\eta\in{\mathcal{U}}_{0}
, one has

|\left\langle\partial_{\eta}l_{1}(\eta,\cdot)-\partial_{\eta}\overline{l}(\eta)\right\rangle|\leq C\rho,\qquad\left\langle(\partial_{\eta}l_{1}(\eta,\cdot)-\partial_{\eta}\overline{l}(\eta))^{2}\right\rangle\leq C\rho\,.\vskip-2.84544pt

(4.80)

Proof.

Differentiating (
4.75
) with respect to
\eta
, we obtain

\partial_{\theta}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi),\psi)\,\partial_{\eta}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi)=\partial_{\theta}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta))\,\partial_{\eta}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)=1,\vskip-5.69046pt

(4.81)

that implies

\partial_{\eta}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi)-\partial_{\eta}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)=-\frac{\partial_{\theta}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi),\psi)-\partial_{\theta}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta))}{\partial_{\theta}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi),\psi)\,\partial_{\theta}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta))}.\vskip-2.84544pt

(4.82)

Thus, we find that square
\bigl(\partial_{\eta}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi)\!-\!\partial_{\eta}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)\bigr)^{2}
is bounded by

\frac{2}{\tau_{1}^{2}\overline{\tau}^{2}}\bigl(\|\partial_{\theta}^{2}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}\bigl({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi)\!-\!\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)\bigr)^{2}+\bigl(\partial_{\theta}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta),\psi)\!-\!\partial_{\theta}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta))\bigr)^{2}\bigr),\vskip-2.84544pt

where
\tau_{1}
and
\overline{\tau}
are on lower bounds, respectively, on
\partial_{\theta}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}
and
\partial_{\theta}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}

(see Subsection
3.2.4
), so that

\big\langle{\bigl(\partial_{\eta}l_{1}-\partial_{\eta}\overline{l}\bigr)^{2}}\big\rangle\leq\frac{2}{\tau_{1}^{2}\overline{\tau}^{2}}\left(\|\partial_{\theta}^{2}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}\|_{\infty}^{2}\big\langle{\bigl(l_{1}-\overline{l}\bigr)^{2}}\big\rangle+\big\langle{\bigl(\partial_{\theta}h_{1}-\partial_{\theta}\overline{h}\bigr)^{2}}\big\rangle\right),\vskip-2.84544pt

which, together with (
4.28b
) and (
4.76
), implies the second of (
4.80
).
Rewriting (
4.82
) as

-\frac{\partial_{\theta}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi),\psi)-\partial_{\theta}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta))}{\bigl(\partial_{\theta}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta))\bigr)^{2}}+\frac{\bigl(\partial_{\theta}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi),\psi)-\partial_{\theta}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta))\bigr)^{2}}{\partial_{\theta}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi),\psi)\,\bigl(\partial_{\theta}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta))\bigr)^{2}},\vskip-5.69046pt

and expanding

\displaystyle\partial_{\theta}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi),\psi)=\partial_{\theta}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta),\psi)+\partial_{\theta}^{2}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta))\,\left(({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi)-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)\right)

\displaystyle+\left(\partial_{\theta}^{2}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta),\psi)-\partial_{\theta}^{2}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta))\right)\,\left(({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi)-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)\right)

\displaystyle+\bigl(({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi)-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)\bigr)^{2}\int_{0}^{1}{\rm d}t\,(1-t)\partial_{\theta}^{3}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}\bigl(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta),\psi)+t\bigl(({\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}(\eta,\psi)-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\eta)\bigr)\bigr),

we obtain eventually

\displaystyle\bigl|\big\langle{\partial_{\eta}l_{1}-\partial_{\eta}\overline{l}(\eta)}\big\rangle\bigr|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\overline{\tau}^{2}}\Bigl(\bigl|\big\langle{\partial_{\theta}h_{1}-\partial_{\theta}\overline{h}}\big\rangle\bigr|+\|\partial_{\theta}^{2}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}\|_{\infty}\bigl|\langle l_{1}-\overline{l}\rangle\bigr|+\bigl(\big\langle{(\partial_{\theta}^{2}h_{1}-\partial_{\theta}^{2}\overline{h})^{2}}\big\rangle\langle(l_{1}-\overline{l})^{2}\rangle\bigr)^{1/2}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{\theta}^{2}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}\|_{\infty}\bigl|\langle(l_{1}-\overline{l})^{2}\rangle\bigr|\Bigr)+\frac{2}{\tau_{1}^{2}\overline{\tau}}\Bigl(\|\partial_{\theta}^{3}{\mathcal{H}}_{1}\|_{\infty}\bigl|\langle(l_{1}-\overline{l})^{2}\rangle\bigr|+\bigl|\big\langle{(\partial_{\theta}h_{1}-\partial_{\theta}\overline{h})^{2}}\big\rangle\bigr|\Bigr),\vskip-2.84544pt

so that the first of (
4.80
) follows from the estimates of Propositions
4.16

and Lemmas
4.45
and
4.48
.
∎

Recalling that
l=l_{1}
, by (
3.13
), Lemmas
4.47
and
4.48
immediately imply the following result.

Proposition 4.49
.

Let
l
be defined as in (
2.15
).
There is a constant
C
such that, for all
\eta\in{\mathcal{U}}_{0}
,

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle l(\eta,\cdot)-\overline{l}(\eta)\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\rho,

\displaystyle\left\langle(l(\eta,\cdot)-\overline{h}(\eta))^{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq C\rho

(4.83a)

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\partial_{\eta}l(\eta,\cdot)-\partial_{\eta}\overline{l}(\eta)\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\rho,

\displaystyle\left\langle(\partial_{\eta}l(\eta,\cdot)-\partial_{\eta}\overline{l}(\eta))^{2}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq C\rho\,.

(4.83b)

4.10
Fluctuations of the dynamics: proof of Theorem
6

We start by proving the following result for the extension of the translated map
{\mathscr{S}}_{1}
.

Lemma 4.50
.

For any
\gamma^{\prime}\in(0,\gamma)

there exists a constant
C
such
that for all
\theta\in{\mathcal{U}}
and all
n\in\mathds{N}

\bigl|\big\langle{({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\cdot)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta)}\big\rangle\bigr|\leq C\rho(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{n},\qquad\bigl\langle\bigl(({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\cdot)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta)\bigr)^{2}\bigr\rangle\leq C\rho\,(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{2n}.

Proof.
From (
2.20
), (
3.10
)
and (
3.12
), we find

\displaystyle({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta)={\mathcal{H}}_{1}^{-1}({\mathscr{S}}_{0}^{n}({\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\theta,\psi),\psi))-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}_{0}^{n}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta),\psi))

(4.84)

\displaystyle=\kappa^{(n)}(\psi)\,{\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\theta,\psi)-\overline{\mu}^{n}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)+\Bigl(\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi)\,{\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\theta,\psi)\bigr)^{2}-\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)^{2}\Bigr)\,\overline{l}\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)\Bigr)

\displaystyle+\Bigl(\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi)\,{\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\theta,\psi)\bigr)^{2}-\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)^{2}\Bigr)\Bigl(l_{1}\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi){\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\theta,\psi),A_{0}^{n}\psi\bigr)-\overline{l}\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)\Bigr)

\displaystyle+\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)^{2}\Bigl(l_{1}\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi){\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\theta,\psi),A_{0}^{n}\psi\bigr)-\overline{l}\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)\Bigr).

Shorten for notational simplicity

\displaystyle{\mathcal{A}}_{n}(\theta,\psi)
