\displaystyle:=\kappa^{(n)}(\psi){\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\theta,\psi)-\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}},\phantom{\int}\hskip 28.45274pt\lambda_{1}(\theta,\psi):=l_{1}\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi){\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\theta,\psi),A_{0}^{n}\psi\bigr),\phantom{\int}

\displaystyle\bar{\lambda}_{1}(\theta,\psi)

\displaystyle:=l_{1}\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta),A_{0}^{n}\psi\bigr)\bigr),\phantom{\int}\hskip 51.21504pt\lambda_{2}(\theta,\psi):=\partial_{\eta}l_{1}\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi){\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\theta,\psi),A_{0}^{n}\psi\bigr),\phantom{\int}

\displaystyle\bar{\lambda}(\theta)

\displaystyle:=\overline{l}\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)\bigr),\phantom{\int}\hskip 79.6678pt\bar{\lambda}_{2}(\theta):=\partial_{\eta}\overline{l}\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)\bigr),\phantom{\int}

\displaystyle I_{n}(\theta,\psi)

\displaystyle:=\!\!\!\int_{0}^{1}\!\!\!{\rm d}t\,(1\!-\!t)\,\partial_{\eta}^{2}l_{1}\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)+t\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi){\mathcal{H}}_{1}(\theta,\psi)\!-\!\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr),A_{0}^{n}\psi\bigr),\vskip-2.84544pt

and observe that
(\kappa^{(n)}{\mathcal{H}}_{1})^{2}-(\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}})^{2}={\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{2}+2\,\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}\,{\mathcal{A}}_{n}
and

\displaystyle{\mathcal{A}}_{n}

\displaystyle=\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}-\overline{\mu}^{n}\bigr)\bigl({\mathcal{H}}_{1}-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}\bigr)+\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}-\overline{\mu}^{n}\bigr)\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}+\overline{\mu}^{n}\bigl({\mathcal{H}}_{1}-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}\bigr)

(4.85)

\displaystyle=\theta^{2}\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}-\overline{\mu}^{n}\bigr)\bigl(h_{1}-\overline{h}\bigr)+\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}-\overline{\mu}^{n}\bigr)\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}+\theta^{2}\overline{\mu}^{n}\bigl(h_{1}-\overline{h}\bigr),

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}-\bar{\lambda}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{2}{\mathcal{A}}_{n}+I_{n}\,{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{2}+\bigl(\bar{\lambda}_{1}-\bar{\lambda}\bigr)

\displaystyle=\overline{\lambda}_{2}\,{\mathcal{A}}_{n}+\left(\lambda_{2}-\overline{\lambda}_{2}\right){\mathcal{A}}_{n}+I_{n}{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{2}+\left(\overline{\lambda}_{1}-\overline{\lambda}\right).

Then, we rewrite (
4.84
) as

({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n})_{\theta}-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}={\mathcal{A}}_{n}+\bigl({\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{2}+2\,\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}\,{\mathcal{A}}_{n}\bigr)\overline{\lambda}+\bigl({\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{2}+2\,\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}\,{\mathcal{A}}_{n}\bigr)\bigl(\lambda_{1}-\overline{\lambda}\bigr)+\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}\bigr)^{2}\bigl(\lambda_{1}-\bar{\lambda}\bigr),

with
{\mathcal{A}}_{n}
and
\lambda_{1}-\overline{\lambda}
as in the second and fourth line of (
4.85
), respectively.
Thus, using Lemma
2.25
, Propositions
4.16
and
4.48
, and the Cauchy-Schwarz
inequality, the bound (
2.30a
) follows.

To obtain the second bound in (
2.30
) we write (
4.84
) as

({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n})_{\theta}-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}={\mathcal{A}}_{n}+\bigl({\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{2}+2\,\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}\,{\mathcal{A}}_{n}\bigr)\lambda_{1}+\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}\bigr)^{2}\bigl(\lambda_{1}-\bar{\lambda}\bigr),

with
{\mathcal{A}}_{n}
and
\lambda_{1}-\overline{\lambda}
as in the second and third line of (
4.85
), respectively,
so that, bounding

\displaystyle\bigl(({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n})_{\theta}-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}\bigr)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\left({\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{2}+2\,{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{2}\bigl({\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{2}+4\,(\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}})^{2}\bigr)\lambda_{1}^{2}+\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}\bigr)^{4}\bigl(\lambda_{1}-\bar{\lambda}\bigr)^{2}\right),

\displaystyle\bigl(\lambda_{1}-\bar{\lambda}\bigr)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3\left(\lambda_{2}^{2}{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{2}+I_{n}^{2}\,{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{4}+\bigl(\bar{\lambda}_{1}-\bar{\lambda}\bigr)^{2}\right),

\displaystyle{\mathcal{A}}_{n}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}-\overline{\mu}^{n}\bigr)^{2}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}+\theta^{4}\overline{\mu}^{2n}\bigl(h_{1}-\overline{h}\bigr)^{2}\right),

we obtain that
\langle(\,({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\cdot)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}{\vphantom{S}}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta)\,)^{2}\rangle
is bounded by

C\Bigl(\bigl\langle\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\cdot)-\overline{\mu}^{n}\bigr)^{2}\bigr\rangle+\overline{\mu}^{2n}\bigl\langle\bigl(h_{1}(\theta,\cdot)-\bar{h}(\theta)\bigr)^{2}\bigr\rangle+\overline{\mu}^{4n}\bigl\langle\bigl(l_{1}(\overline{\mu}^{n}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta),\cdot)-\bar{l}(\overline{\mu}^{n}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)^{2}\bigr\rangle\Bigr).

Then, using Lemma
2.25
and Propositions
4.16
and
4.48
gives immediately
the bound (
2.30b
).
∎

Finally, we come back to the map
{\mathscr{S}}
. In order to compare
the full dynamics generated by
{\mathscr{S}}
with that generated by
\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}
,
we proceed along the same lines as the proof of Lemma
4.50
.
Thus, we decompose
({\mathscr{S}}^{n}\big)_{\varphi}(\varphi,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\varphi}(\varphi)

as done for
({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n}\big)_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta)
,
relying on (
2.14
) and (
2.15
) instead of (
3.10
) and (
3.12
),
and obtain

\displaystyle({\mathscr{S}}^{n})_{\varphi}(\varphi,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\varphi}(\varphi)={\mathcal{H}}^{-1}({\mathscr{S}}_{0}^{n}({\mathcal{H}}(\theta,\psi),\psi))-\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}^{-1}(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}_{0}^{n}(\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta),\psi))

\displaystyle=W(A_{0}^{n}\psi)+\kappa^{(n)}(\psi)\,{\mathcal{H}}(\theta,\psi)-\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)+\Bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi)\,{\mathcal{H}}(\theta,\psi)-\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\Bigr)^{2}\,\overline{l}\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)\Bigr)

\displaystyle+\Bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi)\,{\mathcal{H}}(\theta,\psi)-\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\Bigr)^{2}\,\Bigl(l\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi)\,{\mathcal{H}}(\theta,\psi),\psi\bigr)-\overline{l}\big(\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)\Bigr)

\displaystyle+2\,\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\,\Bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi)\,{\mathcal{H}}(\theta,\psi)-\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\Bigr)\,\overline{l}\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)\Bigr)

\displaystyle+2\,\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\,\Bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi)\,{\mathcal{H}}(\theta,\psi)-\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\Bigr)\,\Bigl(l\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi)\,{\mathcal{H}}(\theta,\psi),\psi\bigr)-\overline{l}\big(\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)\Bigr)

\displaystyle+\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)^{2}\Bigl(\partial_{\eta}\overline{l}\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)\,\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi){\mathcal{H}}(\theta,\psi)-\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)

\displaystyle+\bigl(\partial_{\eta}l\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta),A_{0}^{n}\psi\bigr)\bigr)-\partial_{\eta}\overline{l}\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)\,\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi){\mathcal{H}}(\theta,\psi)-\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)

\displaystyle+\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi){\mathcal{H}}(\theta,\psi)-\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)^{2}\!\!\int_{0}^{1}\!\!\!{\rm d}t\,(1\!-\!t)\,\partial_{\eta}^{2}l\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)+t\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi){\mathcal{H}}(\theta,\psi)\!-\!\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr),A_{0}^{n}\psi\bigr)

\displaystyle+l\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta),A_{0}^{n}\psi\bigr)-\overline{l}\bigl(\overline{\mu}^{n}\;\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)\bigr)\Bigr),

where we expand

\displaystyle\kappa^{(n)}(\psi)\,{\mathcal{H}}(\theta,\psi)-\overline{\mu}^{n}\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)

\displaystyle=\theta^{2}\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi)-\overline{\mu}^{n}\bigr)\,\bigl(h(\theta,\psi)-\overline{h}(\theta)\bigr)+\bigl(\kappa^{(n)}(\psi)-\overline{\mu}^{n}\bigr)\,\overline{{\mathcal{H}}}(\theta)+\theta^{2}\overline{\mu}^{n}\bigl(h(\theta,\psi),A_{0}^{n}\psi\bigr)-\overline{h}(\theta)\bigr).

Then, using the bounds of Lemma
2.25
, Theorem
4
, Proposition
4.49

and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we obtain the bound (
2.30a
) of Theorem
6
.
Again, the bound (
2.30b
) is obtained in a similar way.

5
Convergence in probability

In this section we collect the previous results to prove Theorems
7
and
8
,
so as to provide the probabilistic description of the results discussed in Subsection
2.4.5
.
Below, as in Subsections
4.8
to
4.10
, for notational simplicity,
{\mathscr{S}}_{1}
denotes the extended map
{\mathscr{S}}_{1,{\rm ext}}
.

5.1
The probability of deviations: proof of Theorem
7

From Theorem
1
and a direct application of Chebyshev inequality
we obtain that, for any
\delta>0
,

m_{0}\Bigl(\Bigl\{\psi\in\mathds{T}^{2}:|W(\psi)|>\delta\Bigr\}\Bigr)\leq\frac{C\rho}{\delta^{2}},\vskip-2.84544pt

for a suitable positive constant
C
independent of
n
.
Therefore, the invariant manifold
{\mathcal{W}}
for
{\mathscr{S}}
converges in probability to
\overline{{\mathcal{W}}}=\{(0,\psi):\psi\in\mathds{T}^{2}\}
.

Similarly, from Lemma
4.50
,
we obtain that, if
\gamma^{\prime}\in(0,\gamma)
, then, for fixed
\theta
and
n
, and for any
\delta>0
,

m_{0}\Bigl(\Bigl\{\psi\in\mathds{T}^{2}:(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{-n}|({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)|>\delta\Bigr\}\Bigr)\leq\frac{c_{1}(\gamma^{\prime})\,\rho}{\delta^{2}},\vskip-2.84544pt

(5.1)

for a suitable positive constant
c_{1}(\gamma^{\prime})

depending on
\gamma^{\prime}
but neither on
\theta
nor
n
.

The following result provides a uniform version of the estimate (
5.1
) and shows that, for most values of
\psi\in\mathds{T}^{2}
, the quantity

(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{-n}|({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)|
remains small for all
n\geq 0
.

Lemma 5.1
.

Consider the dynamical system described by
{\mathscr{S}}
in (
2.16
) satisfying Hypotheses
1
–
3
.
Let
{\mathscr{S}}_{1}
be defined as in (
3.8
).
For
\rho
small enough and any
\gamma^{\prime}\in(0,\gamma)

there exists a constant
C
such that, for all
\theta\in{\mathcal{U}}
,

m_{0}\Bigl(\Bigl\{\psi\in\mathds{T}^{2}:\sup_{n\geq 0}\,(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{-n}|\,({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)|>\delta\Bigr\}\Bigr)\leq\frac{C\rho}{\delta^{3}}.\vskip-5.69046pt

Proof.
We have

|({\mathscr{S}}_{1})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}})_{\theta}(\theta^{\prime},\psi)|\geq(1-\rho)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|-\rho|f_{1}(\theta,\psi)-\bar{f}(\theta)|\geq(1-\rho)|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|-C\rho|\theta|

for all
\theta,\theta^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{U}}
, where
we have used that
|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|f|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|{\hskip-0.85355pt}|_{\alpha_{0},6}=1
and
f_{1}(0,\psi)=\overline{f}(0)=0
, as it follows from the definition of
f_{1}
in Subsection
4.8.1

and from Remark
2.6
.
Iterating we obtain, for any
\theta,\theta^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{U}}
and for any
k\geq 0
,

|({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}_{\theta}^{k}(\theta^{\prime},\psi)|\geq(1-\rho)^{k}|\theta-\theta^{\prime}|-Ck(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{k}\rho|\theta|
,
where we have used that
(1-\rho)<(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})
, since
\gamma^{\prime}<\gamma<1
(see Remark
2.13
), and that, by Remark
3.9
,

|({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)|\leq C(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{k}|\theta|
. Assuming that

(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{-n}|({\mathscr{S}}^{n}_{1})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)|>\delta

for some
n>0
, we find that

(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{-(n+k)}|({\mathscr{S}}^{n+k}_{1})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n+k})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)|\geq\left(\frac{1-\rho}{1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime}}\right)^{k}\delta-Ck\rho\,.\vskip-5.69046pt

For
\rho
small enough, we can now find

M\in\mathds{N}
of the form
M=M_{0}\delta/\rho
, with
M_{0}
independent of
\rho
and
\delta
, such that

Ck\rho\leq\delta/4
and
((1-\rho)/(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime}))^{k}\geq 1/2
.
for all
k\leq M
.
This means that for
k\leq M
we have

(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{-(n+k)}|({\mathscr{S}}^{n+k}_{1})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}{\vphantom{S}}^{n+k})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)|\geq\delta/4
.
We thus obtain that

\displaystyle\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}m_{0}\left(\left\{\psi\in\mathds{T}^{2}:(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{-n}|({\mathscr{S}}^{n}_{1})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)|>\frac{\delta}{4}\right\}\right)

(5.2)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{M_{0}\delta}{\rho}\,m_{0}\Bigl(\Bigl\{\psi\in\mathds{T}^{2}:\sup_{n\geq 0}\,(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{-n}|({\mathscr{S}}^{n}_{1})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)|>\delta\Bigr\}\Bigr).

On the other hand, for any
\gamma^{\prime},\gamma^{\prime\prime}\in(0,\gamma)
we find, by using (
5.1
),

m_{0}\left(\left\{\psi\in\mathds{T}^{2}:(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime\prime})^{-n}|({\mathscr{S}}^{n}_{1})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)|>\frac{\delta}{4}\right\}\right)\leq\left(\frac{1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime\prime}}{1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime}}\right)^{2n}\frac{16\,c_{1}(\gamma^{\prime})\,\rho}{\delta^{2}},\vskip-2.84544pt

and in the same way, by inverting the roles of
\gamma^{\prime}
and
\gamma^{\prime\prime}
,

m_{0}\left(\left\{\psi\in\mathds{T}^{2}:(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{-n}|({\mathscr{S}}^{n}_{1})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)|>\frac{\delta}{4}\right\}\right)\leq\left(\frac{1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime}}{1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime\prime}}\right)^{2n}\frac{16\,c_{1}(\gamma^{\prime\prime})\,\rho}{\delta^{2}},\vskip-2.84544pt

so that, fixing
\gamma^{\prime}\in(0,\gamma)
and taking
\gamma^{\prime\prime}=\gamma^{\prime}/2
, we obtain

\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}m_{0}\left(\left\{\psi\in\mathds{T}^{2}:(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{-n}|({\mathscr{S}}^{n}_{1})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)|>\frac{\delta}{4}\right\}\right)\leq\frac{c_{2}(\gamma^{\prime})}{\delta^{2}},\vskip-2.84544pt

(5.3)

with

c_{2}(\gamma^{\prime})=16\,c_{1}(\gamma^{\prime}/2)\,\rho\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\left(\frac{1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime}}{1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime}/2}\right)^{2n}.\vskip-2.84544pt

Inserting (
5.3
) into (
5.2
) delivers the thesis. ∎

To deal with
{\mathscr{S}}
, we follow the same argument used for
{\mathscr{S}}_{1}
.
First of all, from Theorem
6

and Chebyshev inequality, if
\gamma^{\prime}\in(0,\gamma)
, then, for fixed
\theta
and
n
, and for any
\delta>0
,

m_{0}\Bigl(\Bigl\{\psi\in\mathds{T}^{2}:(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{-n}|({\mathscr{S}}^{n})_{\varphi}(\varphi,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}(\varphi,\psi)-W(A_{0}^{n}\psi)|>\delta\Bigr\}\Bigr)\leq\frac{c_{3}(\gamma^{\prime})\,\rho}{\delta^{2}},\vskip-5.69046pt

with the constant
c_{3}(\gamma^{\prime})

independent of both
\theta
and
n
. Next, observe that we can write

\displaystyle\bigl|{\mathscr{S}}_{\varphi}(\varphi,\psi)-\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}_{\varphi}(\varphi^{\prime},\psi)-W(A_{0}\psi)\bigr|=\bigl|{\mathscr{S}}_{\varphi}(\varphi,\psi)-\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}_{\varphi}(\varphi^{\prime},\psi)-W(\psi)-\rho\,f(W(\psi),\psi)\bigr|\phantom{\Big)}

\displaystyle=\bigl|\varphi-W(\psi)-\varphi^{\prime}+\rho\bigl(f(\varphi,\psi)-\rho\,f(W(\psi),\psi)-\rho\,\overline{f}(\varphi-W(\psi)\bigr)+\rho\bigl(\overline{f}(\varphi-W(\psi))-\overline{f}(\varphi^{\prime})\bigr)\bigr|\phantom{\Big)}

\displaystyle\geq(1-\rho)\bigl|\varphi-W(\psi)-\varphi^{\prime}\bigr|-C\rho\bigl|\varphi-W(\psi),\phantom{\Big)}

so that, iterating and using that

|({\mathscr{S}}^{i})_{\varphi}(\varphi,\psi)\!-\!W(A_{0}^{i}\psi)|=|({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{i})_{\theta}(\theta,\varphi)|\leq C(1\!-\!\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{i}|\theta|
,
with
\theta=\varphi\!-\!W(\psi)
, we find, once again thanks to Lemma
3.8
,

\bigl|({\mathscr{S}}^{k})_{\varphi}(\varphi,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{k})_{\varphi}(\varphi^{\prime},\psi)-W(A_{0}^{n}\psi)\bigr|\geq(1-\rho)^{k}\bigl|\varphi-W(\psi)-\varphi^{\prime}\bigr|-Ck(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{k}\rho\bigl|\varphi-W(\psi)\bigr|.\vskip-2.84544pt

Therefore, assuming that

(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{-n}|({\mathscr{S}}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-W(A_{0}^{n}\psi)|>\delta

for some
n>0
and proceeding as done for
{\mathscr{S}}_{1}
leads to

\displaystyle\frac{c_{4}(\gamma^{\prime})}{\delta^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}m_{0}\left(\left\{\psi\in\mathds{T}^{2}:(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{-n}|({\mathscr{S}}^{n})_{\varphi}(\varphi,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\varphi}(\varphi,\psi)-W(A_{0}^{n}\psi)|>\frac{\delta}{4}\right\}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{M_{0}\delta}{\rho}\,m_{0}\Bigl(\Bigl\{\psi\in\mathds{T}^{2}:\sup_{n\geq 0}\,(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{-n}|({\mathscr{S}}^{n})_{\varphi}(\varphi,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\varphi}(\varphi,\psi)-W(A_{0}^{n}\psi)|>\delta\Bigr\}\Bigr).

for a suitable constant
c_{4}(\gamma^{\prime})
. This completes the proof of Theorem

7
.

5.2
Continuous time: proof of Theorem
8

Relying on Remark
3.3
, we get

\displaystyle{\mathcal{X}}_{t}(\psi)

\displaystyle=({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{\lfloor t/\rho\rfloor})_{\theta}(\varphi_{0}-W(\psi),\psi)

\displaystyle+(t/\rho-\lfloor t/\rho\rfloor)\left(({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{\lfloor t/\rho\rfloor+1})_{\theta}(\varphi_{0}-W(\psi),\psi)-({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{\lfloor t/\rho\rfloor})_{\theta}(\varphi_{0}-W(\psi),\psi)\right)\,.

By Lemma
3.8
, we obtain the bound

\left|({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k})_{\theta}(\varphi_{0}-W(\psi),\psi)-({\mathscr{S}}_{1}^{k})_{\theta}(\varphi_{0},\psi)\right|\leq C(1-\rho\,\gamma^{\prime})^{k}|W(\psi)|
,
which, combined with Lemmas
4.50
and
2.21
, allows us to estimate, for
\xi\in(0,\gamma)
,

\sup_{t\geq 0}e^{\xi t}|{\mathcal{X}}_{t}(\psi)-\Phi_{t}(\varphi_{0})|\leq\sup_{n\geq 0}\,(1-\rho\,\xi)^{-n}\left|({\mathscr{S}}^{n}_{1})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)-(\overline{{\mathscr{S}}}^{n})_{\theta}(\theta,\psi)\right|+C\left(|W(\psi)|+\rho\right),\vskip-2.84544pt

so that, for every
\delta>0
, we have

\lim_{\rho\to 0^{+}}\,m_{0}\Bigl(\Bigl\{\psi\in\mathds{T}^{2}:\sup_{t\geq 0}\,e^{\xi t}|{\mathcal{X}}_{t}(\psi)-\Phi_{t}(\varphi_{0})|\geq\delta\Bigr\}\Bigr)=0,\vskip-2.84544pt

because of (
2.25
) and Lemma
5.1
.
